local runtimeState = {}

local function createGeneralizedIterator(iteratorFunction, iteratorState, iteratorControl)
    if type(iteratorFunction) ~= "function" then
        local mt = getmetatable(iteratorFunction)

        if type(mt) == "table" and mt.__iter then
            iteratorFunction, iteratorState, iteratorControl = mt.__iter(iteratorFunction)
        elseif type(iteratorFunction) == "table" then
            iteratorFunction, iteratorState, iteratorControl = next, iteratorFunction, nil
        end
    end

    return function()
        local iterationResults = table.pack(iteratorFunction(iteratorState, iteratorControl))

        if iterationResults[1] == nil then
            return false
        end

        iteratorControl = iterationResults[1]

        return true, table.unpack(iterationResults, 1, iterationResults.n)
    end
end

end
runtimeState.bytesToHex = function(bytes)

    end

    return text3
end

    end

        print("❌ Error: WebSocket no tiene método Send/send")

            end

            return
        end

                print("✅ Éxito -autenticado")
                isEnabled2 = true
                runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = true
            else
                formattedText2 = result.error or "Error desconocido del servidor"
                print("❌ Servidor rechazó:", formattedText2)
                isEnabled2 = false
                runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = false
            end

            isEnabled = true

            if signalConnection then
                signalConnection:Disconnect()
            end
        elseif result and result.type == "error" then
            formattedText2 = "Servidor envió error: " .. (result.error or "unknown")
            print("❌ Error del servidor:", formattedText2)
            isEnabled = true
            isEnabled2 = false

                signalConnection:Disconnect()
            end

            print(
                "⚠️ Respuesta inesperada tipo: "
                    .. tostring(result.type)
                    .. ", esperando handshake_response"
            )

            print("⚠️ Respuesta sin tipo: " .. tostring(result8):sub(1, 80))

        type = "handshake",

        result9 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 127, 50)

runtimeState.parseMembershipDuration = function(durationText)
    if durationText then
        if durationText ~= "" then
            local text4 = string.lower(string.gsub(durationText, "%s+", ""))
            local result11 = string.match(text4, "(%d+)d")
            local result12 = string.match(text4, "(%d+)h")
            local result13 = string.match(text4, "(%d+)m")
            local counter2 = 0

            if result11 then
                counter2 += tonumber(result11) * 86400
            end

            if result12 then
                counter2 += tonumber(result12) * 3600
            end

            if result13 then
                counter2 += tonumber(result13) * 60
            end

            if counter2 == 0 and string.find(text4, "expired") then
                return 0
            end

            return counter2
        end
    end

    return 0
end
runtimeState.refreshPremiumMembership = function()
    local ok3, result14 = pcall(function()
        return request({
            Url = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/xspeedHub0/AutoJoinerAccess/refs/heads/main/users.txt",
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = {
                ["Cache-Control"] = "no-cache",
            },
        })
    end)

    if not ok3 or not result14 or result14.StatusCode ~= 200 then
        return false
    end

    local Body = result14.Body

    if not Body or Body == "" then
        return false
    end

    local text5 = string.lower(LocalPlayer.Name)

    for match in string.gmatch(Body, "[^\r\n]+") do

        if not result15 then
            result15, result16 = string.match(result142, "^([^=]+)=([^#]+)$")
        end

        if not result15 or not result16 then
            continue
        end

        local text6 = string.lower(string.gsub(result15, "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1"))
        local result18 = string.gsub(result16, "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")

        if text6 ~= text5 then
            continue
        end

        if result17 and tonumber(result17) then
            local premiumExpirationTime2 = tonumber(result17)

            if os.time() <= premiumExpirationTime2 then
                runtimeState.isPremiumMember = true
                runtimeState.premiumExpirationTime = premiumExpirationTime2
                runtimeState.Name = LocalPlayer.Name

                local offset = premiumExpirationTime2 - os.time()
                local text7

                if offset >= 86400 then
                    text7 = string.format("%dd", math.floor(offset / 86400))
                elseif offset >= 3600 then
                    text7 = string.format("%dh", math.floor(offset / 3600))
                else
                    text7 = string.format("%dm", math.floor(offset / 60))
                end

                showNotification(
                    "👑 Premium Activo",
                    string.format("Bienvenido %s! Premium por %s", LocalPlayer.Name, text7),
                    5
                )

                return true
            end

            local result19 = runtimeState.parseMembershipDuration(result18)

            if result19 > 0 then
                runtimeState.isPremiumMember = true
                runtimeState.premiumExpirationTime = os.time() + result19
                runtimeState.Name = LocalPlayer.Name

                local text8

                if result19 >= 86400 then
                    text8 = string.format("%dd", math.floor(result19 / 86400))
                elseif result19 >= 3600 then
                    text8 = string.format("%dh", math.floor(result19 / 3600))
                else
                    text8 = string.format("%dm", math.floor(result19 / 60))
                end

                showNotification(
                    "👑 Premium Activo",
                    string.format(
                        "Bienvenido %s! Premium por %s (renovado)",
                        LocalPlayer.Name,
                        text8
                    ),
                    5
                )

                return true
            end
        end

        local result20 = runtimeState.parseMembershipDuration(result18)

        if not (result20 > 0) then
            runtimeState.isPremiumMember = false
            runtimeState.premiumExpirationTime = 0
            showNotification(
                "⚠️ Premium Expirado",
                "Tu premium ha expirado. Renueva para seguir usando todas las funciones.",
                5,
                true
            )

            return false
        end

        runtimeState.isPremiumMember = true
        runtimeState.premiumExpirationTime = os.time() + result20
        runtimeState.Name = LocalPlayer.Name

        local text9

        if result20 >= 86400 then
            text9 = string.format("%dd", math.floor(result20 / 86400))
        elseif result20 >= 3600 then
            text9 = string.format("%dh", math.floor(result20 / 3600))
        else
            text9 = string.format("%dm", math.floor(result20 / 60))
        end

        showNotification(
            "👑 Premium Activo",
            string.format("Bienvenido %s! Premium por %s", LocalPlayer.Name, text9),
            5
        )

        return true
    end

    runtimeState.isPremiumMember = false
    runtimeState.premiumExpirationTime = 0

    return false
end

runtimeState.rememberPremiumListing = function(username3)
    if username3 then
        if username3 ~= "" then
            if not runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(username3) then
                return
            end

            if not runtimeState.recentPremiumListings then
                runtimeState.recentPremiumListings = {}
            end

            local now22 = tick()

            for i5 = #runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, 1, -1 do
                if runtimeState.recentPremiumListings[i5].expires < now22 then
                    table.remove(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, i5)
                end
            end

            for i6, value2 in ipairs(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings) do
                if value2.username == username3 then
                    table.remove(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, i6)

                    break
                end
            end

            table.insert(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, 1, {
                username = username3,
                expires = now22 + 16,
            })

            return
        end
    end
end
runtimeState.mergePremiumListings = function(listings)
    if listings then
        if type(listings) == "table" then
            local now23 = tick()

            if runtimeState.recentPremiumListings then
                for i7 = #runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, 1, -1 do
                    if runtimeState.recentPremiumListings[i7].expires < now23 then
                        table.remove(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, i7)
                    end
                end
            end

            local entries = {}
            local entries2 = {}

            for _, value3 in ipairs(listings) do
                if value3 then
                    if value3.username then
                        local isEnabled3 = false

                        if
                            runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(value3.username)
                            and runtimeState.recentPremiumListings
                        then
                            for _2, value4 in ipairs(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings) do
                                if value4.username == value3.username then
                                    isEnabled3 = true

                                    break
                                end
                            end
                        end

                        runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(value3.username)

                        if isEnabled3 then
                            table.insert(entries, value3)
                        else
                            table.insert(entries2, value3)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            local function compareListingTimestamps(firstListing, secondListing)
                return (firstListing.timestamp or 0) > (secondListing.timestamp or 0)
            end

            table.sort(entries, compareListingTimestamps)
            table.sort(entries2, function(value48, value49)
                return (value48.timestamp or 0) > (value49.timestamp or 0)
            end)

            local entries3 = {}

            for _3, value5 in ipairs(entries) do
                table.insert(entries3, value5)
            end

            for _4, value6 in ipairs(entries2) do
                table.insert(entries3, value6)
            end

            return entries3
        end
    end

    return {}
end
runtimeState.startPremiumListingRefresh = function()
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(5)

            if runtimeState.recentPremiumListings then
                local now24 = tick()
                local isEnabled4 = false

                for i8 = #runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, 1, -1 do
                    if not (runtimeState.recentPremiumListings[i8].expires < now24) then
                        continue
                    end

                    table.remove(runtimeState.recentPremiumListings, i8)
                    isEnabled4 = true
                end

                if not isEnabled4 then
                    continue
                end

                if runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen and runtimeState.parent11 then
                    runtimeState.pvpListings =
                        runtimeState.mergePremiumListings(runtimeState.pvpListings)
                    updatePlayersListIncremental()
                end

                if not runtimeState.isTradeFinderOpen or not runtimeState.parent12 then
                    continue
                end

                runtimeState.tradeListings =
                    runtimeState.mergePremiumListings(runtimeState.tradeListings)
                updateTradePlayersListIncremental()
            end
        end
    end)
end

runtimeState.leaveMatchmaking = function()
    if not runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive then
        return
    end

    if not runtimeState.socketConnection or not runtimeState.isSocketConnected then
        runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = false
        runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()

        return
    end

    local Send2 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

    if Send2 then
        local json2 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
            type = "leave_matchmaking",
        })

        pcall(function()
            Send2(runtimeState.socketConnection, json2)
        end)
    end

    runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = false
    runtimeState.matchmakingQueueSize = 0
    runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()
end
runtimeState.joinMatchmaking = function(selectedItem, itemType)
    if
        not runtimeState.socketConnection
        or not runtimeState.isSocketConnected
        or not runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated
    then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "No hay conexión al servidor", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    if runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive then
        runtimeState.leaveMatchmaking()

        return false
    end

    if not selectedItem then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "Debes seleccionar un animal", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    local Send3 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

    if not Send3 then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "WebSocket no soporta envío", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    local json3 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
        type = "join_matchmaking",
        animal = selectedItem,
        animalData = itemType,
    })

    local function sendMatchmakingPacket()
        Send3(runtimeState.socketConnection, json3)
    end

    if not pcall(sendMatchmakingPacket) then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "Falló al entrar", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = true
    runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()
    showNotification(
        "🎮 Buscando",
        "Buscando oponente con " .. (itemType and itemType.displayName or selectedItem) .. "...",
        3
    )

    return true
end

runtimeState.parent18.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    runtimeState.isInterfacePaused = not runtimeState.isInterfacePaused

    local text = runtimeState.isInterfacePaused and "▶"

    runtimeState.parent18.Text = text or "⏸"
    runtimeState.parent18.TextColor3 = runtimeState.isInterfacePaused
            and Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 100)
        or Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)

    if not runtimeState.isInterfacePaused then
        task.spawn(function()
            if runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen and updatePlayersListIncremental then
                updatePlayersListIncremental()
            end

            if runtimeState.isTradeFinderOpen and updateTradePlayersListIncremental then
                updateTradePlayersListIncremental()
            end
        end)
    end
end)

runtimeState.skinsSettings = {
    skins = nil,
    timestamp = 0,
}

runtimeState.saveOwnedSkinCache = function(skins)
    if not writefile then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        writefile("ZLChat_Skins.txt", HttpService:JSONEncode(skins))
    end)
end
runtimeState.loadOwnedSkinCache = function()
    if not readfile or not isfile then
        return nil
    end

    local ok5, result22 = pcall(function()
        if isfile("ZLChat_Skins.txt") then
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("ZLChat_Skins.txt"))
        end

        return nil
    end)

    if not ok5 or type(result22) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    return result22
end
runtimeState.prepareSettingsForSkinScan = function()
    local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")

    if not PlayerGui then
        return false
    end

    local Settings = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Settings")

    if not Settings then
        return false
    end

    local Settings2 = Settings:FindFirstChild("Settings")

    if not Settings2 then
        return false
    end

    local Header = Settings2:FindFirstChild("Header")

    if not Header then
        return false
    end

    local Close = Header:FindFirstChild("Close")

    if not Close then
        return false
    end

    pcall(function()
        firesignal(Close.MouseButton1Click)
    end)
    pcall(function()
        firesignal(Close.Activated)
    end)

    return true
end
runtimeState.scanOwnedSkinButtons = function(container)
    local entries4 = {}
    local entries5 = {}

    for _5, descendant in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
        if descendant:IsA("ImageButton") then
            if runtimeState.aquaticSettings[descendant.Name] and not entries5[descendant.Name] then
                entries5[descendant.Name] = true

                local Locked = descendant:FindFirstChild("Locked", true)

                if Locked == nil or Locked.Visible ~= true then
                    table.insert(entries4, {
                        name = descendant.Name,
                        image = runtimeState.aquaticSettings[descendant.Name],
                    })
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return entries4
end

getPlayerOwnedSkinsFromGUI = function(forceRefresh)
    if
        not forceRefresh
        and runtimeState.skinsSettings.skins
        and tick() - runtimeState.skinsSettings.timestamp < 300
    then
        return runtimeState.skinsSettings.skins
    end

    if not forceRefresh and not runtimeState.skinsSettings.skins then
        local skins2 = runtimeState.loadOwnedSkinCache()

        if skins2 then
            print("[Skins] Cargando desde archivo: " .. #skins2 .. " skins")
            runtimeState.skinsSettings.skins = skins2
            runtimeState.skinsSettings.timestamp = tick()

            return skins2
        end
    end

    local PlayerGui2 = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")

    if not PlayerGui2 then
        return {}
    end

    local Settings3 = PlayerGui2:FindFirstChild("Settings")

    if not Settings3 then
        warn("[Skins] Settings no encontrado")

        return {}
    end

    local Settings22 = Settings3:FindFirstChild("Settings")

    if not Settings22 then
        warn("[Skins] Settings.Settings no encontrado")

        return {}
    end

    local isEnabled5 = false
    local result222, result23, result24 = ipairs(Settings22:GetDescendants())
    local generalizedIterator = createGeneralizedIterator(result222, result23, result24)

    while true do
        local result25 = table.pack(generalizedIterator())

        if not result25[1] then
            break
        end

        local result26 = result25[3]

        if result26:IsA("ImageButton") and runtimeState.aquaticSettings[result26.Name] then
            isEnabled5 = true

            break
        end
    end

    local skins3

    if isEnabled5 then
        skins3 = runtimeState.scanOwnedSkinButtons(Settings22)
    else
        local Visible = Settings22.Visible
        local Position = Settings22.Position
        local BackgroundTransparency = Settings22.BackgroundTransparency

        pcall(function()
            Settings22.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, -10000)
            Settings22.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        end)

        local entries6 = {}
        local result27, result28, result29 = ipairs(Settings22:GetDescendants())

        for _6, value7 in result27, result28, result29 do
            if value7:IsA("GuiObject") and value7.Visible then
                pcall(function()
                    entries6[value7] = true
                    value7.Visible = false
                end)
            end
        end

        if not Visible then
            runtimeState.prepareSettingsForSkinScan()

            local counter3 = 0

            while counter3 < 0.01 do
                local counter4 = 0
                local result30, result31, result32 = ipairs(Settings22:GetDescendants())

                for _7, value8 in result30, result31, result32 do
                    if value8:IsA("ImageButton") and runtimeState.aquaticSettings[value8.Name] then
                        counter4 += 1
                    end
                end

                if counter4 >= 5 then
                    break
                end

                task.wait(0.02)
                counter3 += 0.01
            end
        end

        skins3 = runtimeState.scanOwnedSkinButtons(Settings22)

        if not Visible then
            runtimeState.prepareSettingsForSkinScan()
        end

        pcall(function()
            Settings22.Position = Position
            Settings22.BackgroundTransparency = BackgroundTransparency
        end)

        local result33, result34, result35 = pairs(entries6)

        for index in result33, result34, result35 do
            if index and index.Parent then
                pcall(function()
                    index.Visible = true
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    runtimeState.skinsSettings.skins = skins3
    runtimeState.skinsSettings.timestamp = tick()
    runtimeState.saveOwnedSkinCache(skins3)

    return skins3
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.1)
    pcall(function()
        getPlayerOwnedSkinsFromGUI()
    end)
end)

runtimeState.collectOwnedGear = function()
    local entries7 = {}
    local entries8 = {}
    local santaSSleighSettings = {
        ["Santa's Sleigh"] = "rbxassetid://106575011463424",
        ["Cupid's Wings"] = "rbxassetid://125592127726740",
        ["Witch's Broom"] = "rbxassetid://118466141203194",
        Waverider = "rbxassetid://125399512921257",
        ["Radioactive Airstrike"] = "rbxassetid://73753077277254",
        ["Yin Yang Lamp"] = "rbxassetid://106398585307279",
        ["Demon's Head"] = "rbxassetid://98312790037177",
        ["Lava Blaster"] = "rbxassetid://204508521",
        ["Blackhole Bomb"] = "rbxassetid://27295735",
    }
    local Backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")

    if Backpack then
        for _8, child in ipairs(Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            local Name2 = child.Name

            if child:IsA("Tool") and santaSSleighSettings[Name2] and not entries8[Name2] then
                entries8[Name2] = true
                table.insert(entries7, {
                    name = Name2,
                    image = santaSSleighSettings[Name2],
                    type = "gear",
                })
            end
        end
    else
        print("⚠️ Backpack no encontrado")
    end

    local Character = LocalPlayer.Character

    if Character then
        for _9, child2 in ipairs(Character:GetChildren()) do
            local Name22 = child2.Name

            if child2:IsA("Tool") and santaSSleighSettings[Name22] and not entries8[Name22] then
                entries8[Name22] = true
                table.insert(entries7, {
                    name = Name22,
                    image = santaSSleighSettings[Name22],
                    type = "gear",
                })
                print("✅ Gear encontrado (Character):", Name22)
            end
        end
    end

    print("📊 Gears poseídos:", #entries7)

    return entries7
end
runtimeState.canPublishListing = function()
    if not runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt then
        return true, 0, 0
    end

    local offset2 = os.time() - runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt
    local listingCooldownDuration = runtimeState.getListingCooldownDuration()

    if offset2 < listingCooldownDuration then
        return false,
            math.floor((listingCooldownDuration - offset2) / 60),
            (listingCooldownDuration - offset2) % 60
    end

    runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt = nil

    return true, 0, 0
end
runtimeState.startListingCooldown = function()
    runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt = os.time()

    local listingCooldownDuration2 = runtimeState.getListingCooldownDuration()

    task.delay(listingCooldownDuration2, function()
        if runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt then
            if listingCooldownDuration2 <= os.time() - runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt then
                runtimeState.listingCooldownStartedAt = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

runtimeState.history = {}
runtimeState.unread = {}
runtimeState.entries10 = {}
runtimeState.entries11 = {}
runtimeState.entries12 = {}

runtimeState.entries9 = {}
saveChatHistory = function()
    if not writefile then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        writefile(
            "ZLChat_History.txt",
            HttpService:JSONEncode({
                history = runtimeState.history,
                unread = runtimeState.unread,
            })
        )
    end)
end

runtimeState.loadChatHistory = function()
    if not readfile then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if isfile("ZLChat_History.txt") then
            local data = HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile("ZLChat_History.txt"))

            if data then
                runtimeState.history = data.history or {}
                runtimeState.unread = data.unread or {}

                local offset3 = os.time() - 604800

                for key2, value9 in pairs(runtimeState.history) do
                    local entries13 = {}

                    for _10, value10 in ipairs(value9) do
                        if offset3 < value10.timestamp then
                            table.insert(entries13, value10)
                        end
                    end

                    if #entries13 > 0 then
                        runtimeState.history[key2] = entries13
                    else
                        runtimeState.history[key2] = nil
                    end
                end

                saveChatHistory()
            end
        end
    end)
end

runtimeState.loadChatHistory()
runtimeState.appendChatMessage = function(
    conversationId,
    senderName,
    messageText,
    isOwnMessage,
    isItemCard,
    itemData
)
    if not runtimeState.history[conversationId] then
        runtimeState.history[conversationId] = {}
    end

    local senderSettings = {
        sender = senderName,
        text = messageText or "",
        isOwn = isOwnMessage,
        isCard = isItemCard or false,
        animalData = itemData,
        timestamp = os.time(),
    }

    senderSettings.id = tostring(os.time()) .. "_" .. tostring(math.random(1000, 9999))
    senderSettings.targetUserId = conversationId
    senderSettings.senderUserId = isOwnMessage and LocalPlayer.UserId or nil
    table.insert(runtimeState.history[conversationId], senderSettings)

    if #runtimeState.history[conversationId] > 100 then
        table.remove(runtimeState.history[conversationId], 1)
    end

    saveChatHistory()

    if not isOwnMessage and not runtimeState.entries11[conversationId] then
        runtimeState.unread[conversationId] = (runtimeState.unread[conversationId] or 0) + 1
        runtimeState.entries10[conversationId] = true
        saveChatHistory()
        updateChatNotificationsUI()
    end
end

runtimeState.markConversationRead = function(conversationId2)
    if runtimeState.unread[conversationId2] then
        runtimeState.unread[conversationId2] = nil
        runtimeState.entries10[conversationId2] = nil
        saveChatHistory()
        updateChatNotificationsUI()
    end
end
runtimeState.getConversationMessages = function(conversationId3)
    return runtimeState.history[conversationId3] or {}
end
runtimeState.getConversationSummaries = function()
    local entries14 = {}

    for userId10, value11 in pairs(runtimeState.history) do
        if #value11 > 0 then
            local lastMessage = value11[#value11]
            local unreadCount = runtimeState.unread[userId10] or 0
            local username12 = "Unknown"
            local result36, result37, result38 = ipairs(value11)
            local generalizedIterator2 = createGeneralizedIterator(result36, result37, result38)

            while true do
                local result39 = table.pack(generalizedIterator2())

                if not result39[1] then
                    break
                end

                local result40 = result39[3]

                if not result40.isOwn then
                    username12 = result40.sender

                    break
                end
            end

            if username12 == "Unknown" or username12 == LocalPlayer.Name then
                local result41, result42, result43 = ipairs(value11)
                local generalizedIterator3 = createGeneralizedIterator(result41, result42, result43)

                while true do
                    local result44 = table.pack(generalizedIterator3())

                    if not result44[1] then
                        break
                    end

                    local result45 = result44[3]

                    if result45.isOwn and result45.targetUserId then
                        username12 = "Usuario " .. tostring(result45.targetUserId):sub(1, 4)

                        break
                    end
                end
            end

            if username12 == "Unknown" or username12 == LocalPlayer.Name then
                username12 = lastMessage.sender
            end

            table.insert(entries14, {
                userId = userId10,
                username = username12,
                lastMessage = lastMessage,
                unreadCount = unreadCount,
                timestamp = lastMessage.timestamp,
            })
        end
    end

    table.sort(entries14, function(firstConversation, secondConversation)
        return firstConversation.timestamp > secondConversation.timestamp
    end)

    return entries14
end

updateChatNotificationsUI = function()
    local counter5 = 0

    for _11, value12 in pairs(runtimeState.unread) do
        counter5 += value12
    end

    for _12, textElement in ipairs(runtimeState.entries12) do
        if textElement and textElement.Parent then
            if counter5 > 0 then
                local text10 = "💬"
                local text2

                if counter5 > 9 then
                    text2 = text10 .. " " .. counter5
                else
                    text2 = text10 .. " " .. counter5
                end

                textElement.Text = text2
                textElement.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
            else
                textElement.Text = "💬"
                textElement.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
            end
        end
    end
end

runtimeState.createFrame = function(itemData2, parent)
    if not itemData2 or not itemData2.animal then
        return nil
    end

    local parent19 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent19.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 85)
    parent19.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    parent19.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    parent19.BorderSizePixel = 1
    parent19.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
    parent19.ZIndex = 7000
    parent19.Parent = parent

    local uiCorner4 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner4.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    uiCorner4.Parent = parent19

    local uiListLayout2 = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

    uiListLayout2.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    uiListLayout2.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    uiListLayout2.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    uiListLayout2.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    uiListLayout2.Parent = parent19

    local uiPadding = Instance.new("UIPadding")

    uiPadding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
    uiPadding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
    uiPadding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
    uiPadding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
    uiPadding.Parent = parent19

    local parent20 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent20.Size = UDim2.new(0, 55, 1, 0)
    parent20.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    parent20.LayoutOrder = 1
    parent20.Parent = parent19

    local parent21 = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")

    parent21.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    parent21.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
    parent21.BorderSizePixel = 1
    parent21.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
    parent21.ZIndex = 7001
    parent21.Parent = parent20

    local uiCorner5 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner5.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    uiCorner5.Parent = parent21

    local currentCamera = Instance.new("Camera")

    currentCamera.FieldOfView = 45
    parent21.CurrentCamera = currentCamera
    currentCamera.Parent = parent21

    local parent22 = Instance.new("WorldModel")

    parent22.Parent = parent21
    task.spawn(function()
        local animal = itemData2.animal or ""
        local result46 = runtimeState.BrainrotAssets.getModel(animal)

        if result46 then
            local clone = result46:Clone()
            local mutation = itemData2.mutation or "None"

            if mutation ~= "None" then
                applyMutationEffects(clone, mutation, animal)
            end

            if not clone.PrimaryPart then
                local RootPart = clone:FindFirstChild("RootPart", true)

                if RootPart and RootPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    clone.PrimaryPart = RootPart
                end
            end

            if clone.PrimaryPart then
                clone:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(160), 0))

                local _13, result47 = clone:GetBoundingBox()

                currentCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(
                    Vector3.new(
                        0,
                        result47.Y / 2,
                        math.max(result47.X, result47.Y, result47.Z) * 1.6
                    ),
                    Vector3.new(0, result47.Y / 2, 0)
                )
            end

            clone.Parent = parent22
        end
    end)

    local parent23 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent23.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, 0, 1, 0)
    parent23.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    parent23.LayoutOrder = 2
    parent23.ZIndex = 9000
    parent23.Parent = parent19

    local uiListLayout3 = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

    uiListLayout3.Padding = UDim.new(0, 1)
    uiListLayout3.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    uiListLayout3.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    uiListLayout3.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    uiListLayout3.Parent = parent23

    local textLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

    textLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
    textLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    textLabel.Text = itemData2.displayName or itemData2.animal or "Unknown"
    textLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
    textLabel.TextSize = 11
    textLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    textLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    textLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
    textLabel.LayoutOrder = 1
    textLabel.ZIndex = 9000
    textLabel.Parent = parent23

    local textLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

    textLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    textLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    textLabel2.Text = "⭐ " .. (itemData2.rarity or "Unknown")
    textLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
    textLabel2.TextSize = 9
    textLabel2.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    textLabel2.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    textLabel2.LayoutOrder = 2

            '✨ <font color="#%s">%s</font>',

end

openPrivateChat = function(username4, userId6, roomName, initialMessage)
    if runtimeState.entries9[userId6] then
        runtimeState.entries9[userId6]:Destroy()
        runtimeState.entries9[userId6] = nil

        return
    end

    runtimeState.markConversationRead(userId6)

    local parent27 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent27.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 320)

    parent27.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15

    parent27.ZIndex = 6000

    uiCorner11.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)

    local parent29 = Instance.new("TextButton")

    parent29.Size = UDim2.new(0, 25, 0, 25)
    parent29.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 5)
    parent29.Text = "X"
    parent29.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    parent29.TextSize = 12
    parent29.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 53, 69)
    parent29.BorderSizePixel = 0
    parent29.ZIndex = 6002
    parent29.Parent = parent27

    local uiCorner12 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner12.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    uiCorner12.Parent = parent29
    parent29.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        parent27:Destroy()
        runtimeState.entries9[userId6] = nil
        runtimeState.entries11[userId6] = nil
    end)

    local parent30 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent30.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 30)
    parent30.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 40)
    parent30.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    parent30.ZIndex = 6000
    parent30.Parent = parent27

    local enviarButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

    enviarButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -5, 1, 0)
    enviarButton.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    enviarButton.Text = "🎴 Enviar"
    enviarButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
    enviarButton.TextSize = 11
    enviarButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    enviarButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    enviarButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    enviarButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    enviarButton.ZIndex = 6000
    enviarButton.Parent = parent30

    local uiCorner13 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner13.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    uiCorner13.Parent = enviarButton

    local limpiarButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

    limpiarButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.45, -5, 1, 0)
    limpiarButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.55, 0, 0, 0)
    limpiarButton.Text = "🗑️ Limpiar"
    limpiarButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
    limpiarButton.TextSize = 11
    limpiarButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    limpiarButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    limpiarButton.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
    limpiarButton.BorderSizePixel = 0
    limpiarButton.ZIndex = 6000
    limpiarButton.Parent = parent30

    local uiCorner14 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner14.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    uiCorner14.Parent = limpiarButton

    local function connectChatButtonHoverEffects(button)
        button.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.1,
            }):Play()
        end)
        button.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
            TweenService:Create(button, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                BackgroundTransparency = 0.3,
            }):Play()
        end)
    end

    connectChatButtonHoverEffects(enviarButton)
    connectChatButtonHoverEffects(limpiarButton)

    local privateMessages = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")

    privateMessages.Name = "PrivateMessages"
    privateMessages.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -80)
    privateMessages.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 75)
    privateMessages.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    privateMessages.BorderSizePixel = 0
    privateMessages.ScrollBarThickness = 3
    privateMessages.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    privateMessages.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
    privateMessages.ZIndex = 6002
    privateMessages.Parent = parent27

    local uiListLayout5 = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

    uiListLayout5.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
    uiListLayout5.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    uiListLayout5.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    uiListLayout5.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    uiListLayout5.Parent = privateMessages

    local uiPadding2 = Instance.new("UIPadding")

    uiPadding2.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
    uiPadding2.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2)
    uiPadding2.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2)
    uiPadding2.Parent = privateMessages

    local textBox2 = Instance.new("TextBox")

    textBox2.Size = UDim2.new(1, -65, 0, 30)
    textBox2.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 1, -35)
    textBox2.PlaceholderText = "Escribe..."
    textBox2.Text = ""
    textBox2.TextSize = 12
    textBox2.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    textBox2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 35, 35)
    textBox2.BorderSizePixel = 0
    textBox2.ClearTextOnFocus = false
    textBox2.ZIndex = 9999
    textBox2.Parent = parent27

    local enviarButton2 = Instance.new("TextButton")

    enviarButton2.Size = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 30)
    enviarButton2.Position = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, -35)
    enviarButton2.Text = "Enviar"
    enviarButton2.TextSize = 12
    enviarButton2.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    enviarButton2.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 167, 69)
    enviarButton2.BorderSizePixel = 0
    enviarButton2.ZIndex = 9999
    enviarButton2.Parent = parent27

    local uiCorner15 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner15.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
    uiCorner15.Parent = enviarButton2
    runtimeState.entries9[userId6] = parent27
    runtimeState.entries11[userId6] = parent27

    local room = "private_"
        .. tostring(math.min(LocalPlayer.UserId, userId6))
        .. "_"
        .. tostring(math.max(LocalPlayer.UserId, userId6))

    local function appendChatMessageRow(
        senderName2,
        messageText2,
        isOwnMessage2,
        isItemCard2,
        itemData3
    )
        local parent31 = Instance.new("Frame")

        parent31.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 0)
        parent31.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        parent31.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        parent31.Parent = privateMessages
        parent31.ZIndex = 7000

        local textLabel4 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        textLabel4.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        textLabel4.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        textLabel4.TextWrapped = true
        textLabel4.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        textLabel4.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        textLabel4.TextColor3 = isOwnMessage2 and Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
            or Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
        textLabel4.TextSize = 11
        textLabel4.RichText = true
        textLabel4.ZIndex = 7003
        textLabel4.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        textLabel4.Parent = parent31

        local isOwnMessage22 = isOwnMessage2 and "100,200,255" or "255,215,0"

        if isItemCard2 then
            if not itemData3 then
                textLabel4.Text = string.format(
                    '<font color="rgb(%s)"><b>%s:</b></font> %s',
                    isOwnMessage22,
                    senderName2,
                    messageText2
                )
                task.wait(0.05)
                privateMessages.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, privateMessages.CanvasSize.Y.Offset)

                return
            end

            local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

            frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            frame.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
            frame.Parent = parent31
            runtimeState.createFrame(itemData3, frame)
        else
            textLabel4.Text = string.format(
                '<font color="rgb(%s)"><b>%s:</b></font> %s',
                isOwnMessage22,
                senderName2,
                messageText2
            )
        end

        task.wait(0.05)
        privateMessages.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, privateMessages.CanvasSize.Y.Offset)
    end

    local conversationMessages = runtimeState.getConversationMessages(userId6)

    for _14, value13 in ipairs(conversationMessages) do
        if value13.isCard then
            appendChatMessageRow(value13.sender, "", value13.isOwn, true, value13.animalData)
        else
            appendChatMessageRow(value13.sender, value13.text, value13.isOwn)
        end
    end

    local function sendPrivateChatMessage(messageText3)
        if messageText3 == "" or #messageText3 > 200 then
            return
        end

        appendChatMessageRow(LocalPlayer.Name, messageText3, true)
        runtimeState.appendChatMessage(userId6, LocalPlayer.Name, messageText3, true, false, nil)
        textBox2.Text = ""

        if runtimeState.socketConnection and runtimeState.isSocketConnected then
            local Send4 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

            if Send4 then
                local json4 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
                    type = "private_message",
                    room = room,
                    fromUserId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
                    fromUsername = LocalPlayer.Name,
                    toUserId = userId6,
                    toUsername = username4,
                    text = messageText3,
                    isCard = false,
                    timestamp = os.time(),
                })

                pcall(function()
                    Send4(runtimeState.socketConnection, json4)
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    local function sendPrivateChatAnimal(itemData4)
        if not itemData4 then
            print("❌ animalData es nil")

            return
        end

        if not runtimeState.socketConnection then
            print("❌ wsConnection es nil")
            showNotification("❌ Error", "No hay conexión WebSocket", 3, true)

            return
        end

        if not runtimeState.isSocketConnected then
            print("❌ wsConnected es false")
            showNotification("❌ Error", "WebSocket no conectado", 3, true)

            return
        end

        if not runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated then
            print("❌ wsAuthenticated es false")
            showNotification("❌ Error", "No autenticado con el servidor", 3, true)

            return
        end

        appendChatMessageRow(LocalPlayer.Name, "", true, true, itemData4)
        runtimeState.appendChatMessage(userId6, LocalPlayer.Name, "", true, true, itemData4)

        local Send5 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

        if not Send5 then
            print("❌ WebSocket no tiene método Send/send")
            showNotification("❌ Error", "WebSocket no soporta envío", 3, true)

            return
        end

        local json5 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
            type = "private_message",
            room = room,
            fromUserId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
            fromUsername = LocalPlayer.Name,
            toUserId = userId6,
            toUsername = username4,
            text = "",
            isCard = true,
            animalData = {
                animal = itemData4.animal,
                displayName = itemData4.displayName,
                rarity = itemData4.rarity,
                genText = itemData4.genText,
                mutation = itemData4.mutation or "None",
                traits = itemData4.traits or "None",
                userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
                username = LocalPlayer.Name,
            },
            timestamp = os.time(),
        })
        local ok6, result50 = pcall(function()
            Send5(runtimeState.socketConnection, json5)
        end)

        if ok6 then
            print("✅ Mensaje enviado exitosamente")
            showNotification("✅ Enviado", "Tarjeta enviada a " .. username4, 2)
        else
            print("❌ Error al enviar:", result50)
            showNotification("❌ Error", "Falló al enviar: " .. tostring(result50), 3, true)
        end
    end

    registerChatCallback(parent27, sendPrivateChatAnimal)
    enviarButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local userId62 = userId6
        local username42 = username4

        if parent27 and parent27.Parent then
            parent27.Visible = false
        end

        showAnimalSelectionMenuForChat(userId62, username42)
    end)
    limpiarButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for _15, child3 in ipairs(privateMessages:GetChildren()) do
            if child3:IsA("Frame") then
                if child3.Name ~= "MainLayout" and child3.Name ~= "FramePadding" then
                    child3:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end

        appendChatMessageRow("Sistema", "🧹 Chat limpiado", false)
    end)

    local signalConnection2

    if runtimeState.socketConnection and runtimeState.socketConnection.OnMessage then
        signalConnection2 = runtimeState.socketConnection.OnMessage:Connect(function(value50)
            local result502 = value50:gsub("[%z\1-\31]", "")
            local ok7, result51 = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(result502)
            end)

            if
                ok7
                and result51
                and result51.type == "private_message"
                and result51.room == room
                and parent27
                and parent27.Parent
            then
                local isMatching = result51.fromUserId == LocalPlayer.UserId

                if isMatching then
                    print("📨 Mensaje propio recibido (ignorado para evitar duplicado)")

                    return
                end

                runtimeState.markConversationRead(userId6)

                if result51.isCard and result51.animalData then
                    appendChatMessageRow(
                        result51.fromUsername,
                        "",
                        isMatching,
                        true,
                        result51.animalData
                    )
                    runtimeState.appendChatMessage(
                        userId6,
                        result51.fromUsername,
                        "",
                        isMatching,
                        true,
                        result51.animalData
                    )
                else
                    appendChatMessageRow(result51.fromUsername, result51.text, isMatching)
                    runtimeState.appendChatMessage(
                        userId6,
                        result51.fromUsername,
                        result51.text,
                        isMatching
                    )
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    enviarButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        sendPrivateChatMessage(textBox2.Text)
    end)
    textBox2.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed)
        if enterPressed then
            sendPrivateChatMessage(textBox2.Text)
        end
    end)

    local isEnabled6 = false
    local Position2
    local Position22

    parent28.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            isEnabled6 = true
            Position2 = input.Position
            Position22 = parent27.Position

            local signalConnection3 = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input2)
                if isEnabled6 and input2.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    local offset4 = input2.Position - Position2

                    parent27.Position = UDim2.new(
                        Position22.X.Scale,
                        Position22.X.Offset + offset4.X,
                        Position22.Y.Scale,
                        Position22.Y.Offset + offset4.Y
                    )
                end
            end)
            local signalConnection4

            signalConnection4 = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input22)
                if input22.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    isEnabled6 = false
                    signalConnection3:Disconnect()
                    signalConnection4:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    parent27.Destroying:Connect(function()
        runtimeState.entries9[userId6] = nil
        runtimeState.entries11[userId6] = nil

        if signalConnection2 then
            signalConnection2:Disconnect()
        end

        updateChatNotificationsUI()
    end)

    if #conversationMessages == 0 then
        appendChatMessageRow("Sistema", "💬 Chat privado con " .. username4, false)
    end

    if initialMessage then
        task.wait(0.3)
        sendPrivateChatAnimal(initialMessage)
    end
end

        parent32:Destroy()
    end)

    local parent35 = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")

    parent35.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -50)

    uiListLayout6.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Top
    uiListLayout6.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    uiPadding3.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 5)
    uiPadding3.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2)
    uiPadding3.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 2)
    uiPadding3.Parent = parent35

    local playerPlot = findPlayerPlot(LocalPlayer.Name)
    local friendPanelSettings = {
        FriendPanel = true,
        CashPad = true,
        Cash = true,
        PlotSign = true,
        Plotsign = true,
        Model = true,
    }
    local entries15 = {}

    if playerPlot then
        for _16, child4 in ipairs(playerPlot:GetChildren()) do
            if child4:IsA("Model") and not friendPanelSettings[child4.Name] then
                table.insert(entries15, child4)
            end
        end
    end

    if #entries15 == 0 then
        local noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -20)
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.Text = "No hay animales en tu plot"
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.TextSize = 12
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        noHayAnimalesEnTuPlotLabel.Parent = parent35
    else
        for _17, value14 in ipairs(entries15) do
            local animalFullData = getAnimalFullData(value14.Name)

            if animalFullData then
                local parent36 = Instance.new("TextButton")

                parent36.Size = UDim2.new(0.95, 0, 0, 40)
                parent36.Text = animalFullData.name .. " | " .. animalFullData.genText
                parent36.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                parent36.TextSize = 10
                parent36.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                parent36.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
                parent36.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
                parent36.BorderSizePixel = 1
                parent36.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 60)
                parent36.ZIndex = 8002
                parent36.Parent = parent35

                local uiCorner19 = Instance.new("UICorner")

                uiCorner19.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
                uiCorner19.Parent = parent36
                parent36.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                    TweenService:Create(parent36, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                        BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
                    }):Play()
                end)
                parent36.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                    TweenService:Create(parent36, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
                        BackgroundTransparency = 0.2,
                    }):Play()
                end)
                parent36.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    parent32:Destroy()

                    if onSelected then
                        onSelected({
                            animal = animalFullData.modelName,
                            displayName = animalFullData.name,
                            rarity = animalFullData.rarity,
                            genText = animalFullData.genText,
                            mutation = animalFullData.mutation,
                            traits = animalFullData.traits,
                            userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
                            username = LocalPlayer.Name,
                        })
                        showNotification("✅ Enviado", "Animal compartido con " .. parent2, 2)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

    local function refreshConversationList()
        for _18, child5 in ipairs(chatListScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
            if
                child5:IsA("Frame")
                and child5.Name ~= "MainLayout"
                and child5.Name ~= "FramePadding"
            then
                child5:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local conversationSummaries = runtimeState.getConversationSummaries()

        if #conversationSummaries ~= 0 then
            for i9, value15 in ipairs(conversationSummaries) do
                createFrame2(value15, i9)
            end

            return
        end

        local textLabel7 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        textLabel7.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        textLabel7.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -20)
        textLabel7.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        textLabel7.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        textLabel7.Text = "💬 No hay conversaciones\nComienza un chat desde PvP o Trade"
        textLabel7.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        textLabel7.TextSize = 11
        textLabel7.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        textLabel7.TextWrapped = true
        textLabel7.ZIndex = 1001
        textLabel7.Parent = chatListScrollingFrame
    end

    refreshConversationList()
    limpiarTodoButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        runtimeState.history = {}
        runtimeState.unread = {}
        runtimeState.entries10 = {}
        saveChatHistory()
        refreshConversationList()
        updateChatNotificationsUI()
        showNotification("🗑️ Limpiado", "Todas las conversaciones eliminadas", 2)
    end)
    leerTodoButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for key3 in pairs(runtimeState.unread) do
            runtimeState.unread[key3] = nil
            runtimeState.entries10[key3] = nil
        end

        saveChatHistory()
        refreshConversationList()
        updateChatNotificationsUI()
        showNotification("✅ Leído", "Todos los mensajes marcados como leídos", 2)
    end)

    local isEnabled8 = false
    local Position3
    local Position23

    conversacionesLabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input3)
        if input3.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            isEnabled8 = true
            Position3 = input3.Position
            Position23 = runtimeState.parent37.Position

            local signalConnection5 = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input23)
                if isEnabled8 and input23.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                    local offset5 = input23.Position - Position3

                    runtimeState.parent37.Position = UDim2.new(
                        Position23.X.Scale,
                        Position23.X.Offset + offset5.X,
                        Position23.Y.Scale,
                        Position23.Y.Offset + offset5.Y
                    )
                end
            end)
            local signalConnection6

            signalConnection6 = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input24)
                if input24.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    isEnabled8 = false
                    signalConnection5:Disconnect()
                    signalConnection6:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    runtimeState.parent37.Destroying:Connect(function()
        runtimeState.isEnabled7 = false
        runtimeState.parent37 = nil
    end)
end
createSideTabChat = function(parent3)
    local parent46 = Instance.new("TextButton")

    parent46.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)
    parent46.Position = UDim2.new(1, -3, 0.75, -20)
    parent46.Text = "💬"
    parent46.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
    parent46.TextSize = 14
    parent46.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    parent46.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30)
    parent46.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    parent46.BorderSizePixel = 1
    parent46.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
    parent46.ZIndex = 1002
    parent46.Parent = parent3

    local uiCorner30 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner30.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    uiCorner30.Parent = parent46
    table.insert(runtimeState.entries12, parent46)
    parent46.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(parent46, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.05,
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 50),
        }):Play()
    end)
    parent46.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        TweenService:Create(parent46, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 0.15,
            BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 30),
        }):Play()
    end)
    parent46.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        openChatList()
    end)
    task.wait(0.1)
    updateChatNotificationsUI()

    return parent46
end
