runtimeState.applyAnimalMutationAppearance = function(animalModel, animalName, mutationName)
    if not mutationName or mutationName == "None" then
        return
    end

    local Mutations

    pcall(function()
        Mutations = require(runtimeState.replicatedStorage3.Datas.Mutations)
    end)

    local MutationSurfaces = runtimeState.replicatedStorage3.MutationSurfaces
        and runtimeState.replicatedStorage3.MutationSurfaces:FindFirstChild(animalName)
    local Vfx = runtimeState.replicatedStorage3.Vfx
        and runtimeState.replicatedStorage3.Vfx:FindFirstChild(mutationName)

    if mutationName == "Rainbow" then
        animalModel:AddTag("RainbowModel")
    else
        local result512 = runtimeState.diamondSettings2[mutationName]

        if result512 then
            for _19, parent47 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if parent47:IsA("BasePart") and not parent47:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        local MaterialVariant = parent47.MaterialVariant

                        if
                            MaterialVariant == "Strawberry Stud Light"
                            or MaterialVariant == "Strawberry Stud Dark"
                        then
                            parent47.MaterialVariant = mutationName .. " Strawberry Stud Light"

                            return
                        end

                        local attribute = parent47:GetAttribute(("%*Color"):format(mutationName))
                            or parent47:GetAttribute("Color")
                        local color = result512[math.clamp(
                            tonumber(attribute or 1) or 1,
                            1,
                            #result512
                        )] or result512[1]

                        if not color then
                            return
                        end

                        local SurfaceAppearance =
                            parent47:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")

                        if SurfaceAppearance then
                            SurfaceAppearance:Destroy()

                            if MutationSurfaces then
                                local clone2 = MutationSurfaces:Clone()

                                if mutationName == "Divine" then
                                    clone2.Color = result512[1] or color
                                else
                                    clone2.Color = color
                                end

                                clone2.Parent = parent47
                            end
                        else
                            parent47.Color = color
                        end

                        if parent47:GetAttribute("Neon") then
                            parent47.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end

        if mutationName == "Galaxy" then
            for _20, descendant2 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant2:IsA("BasePart") and not descendant2:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if
                            (
                                descendant2:GetAttribute("GalaxyColor")
                                or descendant2:GetAttribute("Color")
                                or 1
                            ) == 1
                        then
                            descendant2.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end

                        descendant2.MaterialVariant = "Galaxy Stud"
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "Lava" then
            for _21, descendant3 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant3:IsA("BasePart") and not descendant3:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if
                            (
                                descendant3:GetAttribute("LavaColor")
                                or descendant3:GetAttribute("Color")
                                or 1
                            ) == 1
                        then
                            descendant3.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "YinYang" then
            for _22, descendant4 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant4:IsA("BasePart") and not descendant4:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        local attribute2 = descendant4:GetAttribute("YinYangColor")

                        attribute2 = attribute2 or descendant4:GetAttribute("Color") or 1

                        if attribute2 == 3 or attribute2 == 4 then
                            descendant4.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "Divine" then
            local attribute3 = animalModel:GetAttribute("EmissiveStrength") or 2

            for _23, descendant5 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant5:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                    pcall(function()
                        descendant5.EmissiveStrength = attribute3
                    end)
                end

                if descendant5:IsA("BasePart") and not descendant5:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        local attribute22 = descendant5:GetAttribute("DivineColor")
                            or descendant5:GetAttribute("Color")
                            or 1

                        if attribute22 == 2 then
                            descendant5.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end

                        if not descendant5:GetAttribute("Divine*MaterialMode") then
                            animalModel:GetAttribute("Divine*MaterialMode")
                        end

                        if descendant5:GetAttribute("Divine*Stud") == false then
                            descendant5.MaterialVariant = ""
                        elseif
                            descendant5.MaterialVariant == "Custom Stud"
                            or descendant5:GetAttribute("Divine*Stud") == true
                        then
                            descendant5.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            descendant5.MaterialVariant = "Divine Stud"
                        elseif attribute22 ~= 2 and attribute22 ~= 6 then
                            descendant5.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            descendant5.MaterialVariant = "Divine Stud"
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "Radioactive" then
            for _24, descendant6 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant6:IsA("BasePart") and not descendant6:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        local attribute4 = descendant6:GetAttribute("RadioactiveColor")
                            or descendant6:GetAttribute("Color")
                            or 1

                        if attribute4 == 2 then
                            descendant6.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end

                        if descendant6:GetAttribute("Radioactive*Stud") == false then
                            descendant6.MaterialVariant = ""
                        elseif
                            descendant6.MaterialVariant == "Custom Stud"
                            or descendant6:GetAttribute("Radioactive*Stud") == true
                        then
                            descendant6.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            descendant6.MaterialVariant = "Radioactive Stud"
                        elseif attribute4 ~= 2 and attribute4 ~= 6 then
                            descendant6.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            descendant6.MaterialVariant = "Radioactive Stud"
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "Crystal" then
            for _25, descendant7 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant7:IsA("BasePart") and not descendant7:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                    pcall(function()
                        local attribute5 = descendant7:GetAttribute("CrystalColor")
                            or descendant7:GetAttribute("Color")
                            or 1

                        if attribute5 == 2 then
                            descendant7.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        end

                        if descendant7:GetAttribute("Crystal*Stud") == false then
                            descendant7.MaterialVariant = ""
                        elseif
                            descendant7.MaterialVariant == "Custom Stud"
                            or descendant7:GetAttribute("Radioactive*Stud") == true
                        then
                            descendant7.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            descendant7.MaterialVariant = "Crystal Stud"
                        elseif attribute5 ~= 2 and attribute5 ~= 6 then
                            descendant7.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            descendant7.MaterialVariant = "Crystal Stud"
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "Cursed" then
            for _26, descendant8 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if descendant8:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if not descendant8:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor") then
                        pcall(function()
                            local attribute6 = descendant8:GetAttribute("CursedColor")
                                or descendant8:GetAttribute("Color")
                                or 1

                            if attribute6 == 2 then
                                descendant8.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                            end

                            if descendant8:GetAttribute("Cursed*Stud") == false then
                                descendant8.MaterialVariant = ""
                            elseif
                                descendant8.MaterialVariant == "Custom Stud"
                                or descendant8:GetAttribute("Cursed*Stud") == true
                                or attribute6 ~= 2 and attribute6 ~= 6
                            then
                                descendant8.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                                descendant8.MaterialVariant = "Cursed Stud"
                                descendant8.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 23, 23)
                            end

                            local SurfaceAppearance2 =
                                descendant8:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")

                            if SurfaceAppearance2 then
                                if not descendant8:GetAttribute("Cursed*IgnoreSurfaceColor") then
                                    SurfaceAppearance2.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 23, 23)
                                end

                                if descendant8:GetAttribute("IgnoreSurface") then
                                    SurfaceAppearance2:Destroy()
                                end
                            end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif mutationName == "Cyber" then
            for _27, parent48 in animalModel:GetDescendants() do
                if
                    parent48:IsA("BasePart")
                    and parent48.Transparency ~= 1
                    and not parent48:GetAttribute("IgnoreColor")
                then
                    pcall(function()
                        local attribute7 = parent48:GetAttribute("Cyber*Color")
                            or parent48:GetAttribute("Color")
                        local numericValue = tonumber(attribute7 or 1) or 1
                        local SurfaceAppearance3 =
                            parent48:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")

                        if parent48:GetAttribute("Eyes") then
                            parent48.Color = Color3.fromRGB(62, 155, 255)
                            parent48.Transparency = 0.25
                            parent48.Material = Enum.Material.Neon

                            return
                        end

                        if numericValue == 7 then
                            parent48.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                        elseif numericValue == 4 then
                            parent48.Transparency = 0.5
                            parent48.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                            parent48.MaterialVariant = "Tech Stud"
                            parent48.Color = Color3.fromRGB(62, 155, 255)
                        elseif numericValue == 3 then
                            parent48.Material = Enum.Material.Glass
                            parent48.Transparency = 0.5

                            if not SurfaceAppearance3 and parent48.ClassName == "MeshPart" then
                                Instance.new("SurfaceAppearance").Parent = parent48
                            end
                        elseif numericValue == 1 then
                            parent48.Material = Enum.Material.Glass
                            parent48.Transparency = 0.25

                            if not SurfaceAppearance3 and parent48.ClassName == "MeshPart" then
                                Instance.new("SurfaceAppearance").Parent = parent48
                            end
                        end

                        local SurfaceAppearance22 =
                            parent48:FindFirstChildOfClass("SurfaceAppearance")

                        if SurfaceAppearance22 then
                            local scaledValue = parent48.Size.X * parent48.Size.Y * parent48.Size.Z

                            parent48.Transparency = 0
                            parent48.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
                            SurfaceAppearance22.AlphaMode = Enum.AlphaMode.Overlay
                            SurfaceAppearance22.EmissiveTint = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

                            if scaledValue > 3 then
                                SurfaceAppearance22.Color = Color3.fromRGB(35, 75, 115)
                                SurfaceAppearance22.EmissiveStrength = 50
                            else
                                SurfaceAppearance22.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 25, 30)
                                SurfaceAppearance22.EmissiveStrength = 25
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if Vfx then
        local VfxInstance = animalModel:FindFirstChild("VfxInstance")

        if VfxInstance then
            for _28, child22 in Vfx:GetChildren() do
                pcall(function()
                    child22:Clone().Parent = VfxInstance
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

applyMutationEffects = function(animalModel2, mutationName2, animalName2)
    if mutationName2 then
        if mutationName2 ~= "None" then
            runtimeState.applyAnimalMutationAppearance(
                animalModel2,
                animalName2 or animalModel2.Name,
                mutationName2
            )

            return
        end
    end
end

runtimeState.createTraitBadges = function(traits2, parent4)
    if not traits2 or type(traits2) ~= "table" or #traits2 == 0 then
        return
    end

    local parent49 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent49.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 22)
    parent49.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    parent49.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    parent49.ZIndex = 2000
    parent49.Parent = parent4

    local uiListLayout12 = Instance.new("UIListLayout")

    uiListLayout12.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    uiListLayout12.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    uiListLayout12.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    uiListLayout12.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    uiListLayout12.Parent = parent49

    for _29, value16 in ipairs(traits2) do
        local result52 = runtimeState.ballSettings[value16]

        if result52 and result52.Icon then
            local parent50 = Instance.new("ImageLabel")

            parent50.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
            parent50.Image = result52.Icon
            parent50.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            parent50.ZIndex = 2000
            parent50.Parent = parent49

            local parent51 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

            parent51.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 22)
            parent51.Position = UDim2.new(0, -40, 1, 2)
            parent51.Text = result52.Display
            parent51.TextSize = 10
            parent51.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            parent51.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            parent51.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            parent51.BorderSizePixel = 0
            parent51.Visible = false
            parent51.ZIndex = 3000
            parent51.Parent = parent50

            local uiCorner31 = Instance.new("UICorner")

            uiCorner31.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
            uiCorner31.Parent = parent51
            parent50.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
                parent51.Visible = true
                parent51.ZIndex = 3000

                if parent51.Parent then
                    parent51.Parent.ZIndex = 3000
                end
            end)
            parent50.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
                parent51.Visible = false
            end)
        end
    end
end

runtimeState.Net = game.ReplicatedStorage.Packages.Net
runtimeState.scannedSettings = {
    scanned = false,
    duelRemote = nil,
    tradeRemote = nil,
}

runtimeState.matchesRemoteServiceName = function(remoteName)
    if remoteName then
        if type(remoteName) == "string" then
            local match3 = remoteName:match("^RF/(.+)$")

            match3 = match3 or remoteName

            return #match3 >= 32 and not match3:find("/")
        end
    end

    return false
end
runtimeState.findServiceRemote = function(serviceName)
    local children = runtimeState.Net:GetChildren()
    local i32

    for i10, child6 in ipairs(children) do
        if child6.Name == "RF/" .. serviceName then
            i32 = i10

            break
        end
    end

    if not i32 then
        return nil
    end

    for i11 = 1, 5 do
        local offset6 = i32 - i11

        if offset6 >= 1 then
            local result53 = children[offset6]

            if
                result53
                and result53:IsA("RemoteFunction")
                and runtimeState.matchesRemoteServiceName(result53.Name)
            then
                return result53
            end
        end

        local combinedValue = i32 + i11

        if combinedValue <= #children then
            local result54 = children[combinedValue]

            if
                result54
                and result54:IsA("RemoteFunction")
                and runtimeState.matchesRemoteServiceName(result54.Name)
            then
                return result54
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end
runtimeState.cacheInvitationRemotes = function()
    if runtimeState.scannedSettings.scanned then
        return true
    end

    runtimeState.scannedSettings.duelRemote =
        runtimeState.findServiceRemote("uelsMachineService/Invite")
    runtimeState.scannedSettings.tradeRemote = runtimeState.findServiceRemote("radeService/Invite")
    runtimeState.scannedSettings.scanned = true

    if runtimeState.scannedSettings.duelRemote then
        print("✅ Duel Remote guardado:", runtimeState.scannedSettings.duelRemote.Name)
    end

    if runtimeState.scannedSettings.tradeRemote then
        print("✅ Trade Remote guardado:", runtimeState.scannedSettings.tradeRemote.Name)
    end

    return runtimeState.scannedSettings.duelRemote ~= nil
        or runtimeState.scannedSettings.tradeRemote ~= nil
end
runtimeState.sendDuelInvitationThroughGui = function(username5)
    if not username5 or username5 == "" then
        return false, "Nombre de usuario inválido"
    end

    local ok8, result55 = pcall(function()
        local PlayerGui3 = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 2)

        if not PlayerGui3 then
            return false, "PlayerGui no encontrado"
        end

        local DuelsMachinePlayerList = PlayerGui3:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachinePlayerList")

        if not DuelsMachinePlayerList then
            return false, "GUI de Duelos no encontrada"
        end

        local DuelsMachinePlayerList2 =
            DuelsMachinePlayerList:FindFirstChild("DuelsMachinePlayerList")

        if not DuelsMachinePlayerList2 then
            return false, "Contenedor de Duelos no encontrado"
        end

        local SearchFrame = DuelsMachinePlayerList2:FindFirstChild("SearchFrame")

        SearchFrame = SearchFrame and SearchFrame:FindFirstChild("SearchBox")

        if not SearchFrame or not SearchFrame:IsA("TextBox") then
            return false, "Caja de búsqueda no encontrada"
        end

        local GlobalList = DuelsMachinePlayerList2:FindFirstChild("GlobalList")

        if not GlobalList then
            return false, "Lista de jugadores no encontrada"
        end

        SearchFrame.Text = ""
        task.wait(0.2)
        SearchFrame.Text = username5
        pcall(function()
            firesignal(SearchFrame.FocusLost, true)
        end)
        task.wait(1.5)

        local text112 = string.lower(username5)

        for _30, child7 in ipairs(GlobalList:GetChildren()) do
            if not child7.Name then
                continue
            end

            local Name23 = child7.Name

            if string.lower(Name23:match("^(.+)_%d+$") or Name23) ~= text112 then
                continue
            end

            local Send6
            local Fill = child7:FindFirstChild("Fill")

            if Fill then
                Send6 = Fill:FindFirstChild("Send")
            end

            if not Send6 or not Send6:IsA("ImageButton") and not Send6:IsA("TextButton") then
                print("[Duel] ERROR: Send button no encontrado en", child7.Name)

                if Fill then
                    for _31 in ipairs(Fill:GetChildren()) do
                    end
                end

                return false, "Jugador no encontrado en la lista"
            end

            firesignal(Send6.MouseButton1Click)
            firesignal(Send6.Activated)

            return true, "Invitación enviada"
        end

        return false, "Jugador no encontrado en la lista"
    end)

    if not ok8 then
        return false, "Error en fallback: " .. tostring(result55)
    end

    return true, "Éxito"
end
runtimeState.sendTradeInvitationThroughGui = function(username6)
    if not username6 or username6 == "" then
        return false, "Nombre de usuario inválido"
    end

    local ok9, result56 = pcall(function()
        local PlayerGui4 = Players.LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 2)

        if not PlayerGui4 then
            return false, "PlayerGui no encontrado"
        end

        local TradePlayerList = PlayerGui4:FindFirstChild("TradePlayerList")

        if not TradePlayerList then
            return false, "GUI de Trades no encontrada"
        end

        local TradePlayerList2 = TradePlayerList:FindFirstChild("TradePlayerList")

        if not TradePlayerList2 then
            return false, "Contenedor de Trades no encontrado"
        end

        local Sections = TradePlayerList2:FindFirstChild("Sections")

        if not Sections then
            return false, "Sección de Trades no encontrada"
        end

        local Players2 = Sections:FindFirstChild("Players")

        if not Players2 then
            return false, "Sección Players no encontrada"
        end

        local SearchFrame2 = Players2:FindFirstChild("SearchFrame")

        SearchFrame2 = SearchFrame2 and SearchFrame2:FindFirstChild("SearchBox")

        if not SearchFrame2 or not SearchFrame2:IsA("TextBox") then
            return false, "Caja de búsqueda no encontrada"
        end

        local List = Players2:FindFirstChild("List")

        if not List then
            return false, "Lista de jugadores no encontrada"
        end

        SearchFrame2.Text = ""
        task.wait(0.2)
        SearchFrame2.Text = username6
        pcall(function()
            firesignal(SearchFrame2.FocusLost, true)
        end)
        task.wait(1.5)

        local text12 = string.lower(username6)

        for _32, child8 in ipairs(List:GetChildren()) do
            if not child8.Name then
                continue
            end

            local Name24 = child8.Name

            if string.lower(Name24:match("^(.+)_%d+$") or Name24) ~= text12 then
                continue
            end

            local Send7
            local Fill2 = child8:FindFirstChild("Fill")

            if Fill2 then
                Send7 = Fill2:FindFirstChild("Send")
            end

            if not Send7 or not Send7:IsA("ImageButton") and not Send7:IsA("TextButton") then
                print("[Trade] ERROR: Send button no encontrado en", child8.Name)

                if Fill2 then
                    for _33 in ipairs(Fill2:GetChildren()) do
                    end
                end

                return false, "Jugador no encontrado en la lista"
            end

            firesignal(Send7.MouseButton1Click)
            firesignal(Send7.Activated)

            return true, "Solicitud enviada"
        end

        return false, "Jugador no encontrado en la lista"
    end)

    if not ok9 then
        return false, "Error en fallback: " .. tostring(result56)
    end

    return true, "Éxito"
end
runtimeState.sendDuelInvitationOptimized = function(userId72, username7)
    if not runtimeState.scannedSettings.scanned then
        runtimeState.cacheInvitationRemotes()
    end

    if runtimeState.scannedSettings.duelRemote then
        local ok10 = pcall(function()
            return runtimeState.scannedSettings.duelRemote:InvokeServer(userId72)
        end)

        if ok10 then
            showNotification("⚔️ Duel", string.format("Enviada a %s", username7), 3, false)

            return true
        end
    end

    local result552, result562 = runtimeState.sendDuelInvitationThroughGui(username7)

    if not result552 then
        showNotification("❌ Duel Failed", result562, 4, true)

        return false
    end

    showNotification("⚔️ Duel", string.format("Enviada a %s", username7), 3, false)

    return true
end
runtimeState.sendTradeRequestOptimized = function(userId8, username8)
    if not runtimeState.scannedSettings.scanned then
        runtimeState.cacheInvitationRemotes()
    end

    if runtimeState.scannedSettings.tradeRemote then
        local ok11 = pcall(function()
            return runtimeState.scannedSettings.tradeRemote:InvokeServer(
                "afb005f9-6e81-4e0a-8bb0-3555938a9658",
                userId8
            )
        end)

        if ok11 then
            showNotification("📦 Trade", string.format("Enviada a %s", username8), 3, false)

            return true
        end
    end

    local result57, result58 = runtimeState.sendTradeInvitationThroughGui(username8)

    if result57 then
        showNotification("📦 Trade", string.format("Enviada a %s", username8), 3, false)

        return true
    end

    showNotification("❌ Trade Failed", result58, 4, true)

    return false
end

_G.sendDuelInvitationOptimized = runtimeState.sendDuelInvitationOptimized
_G.sendTradeRequestOptimized = runtimeState.sendTradeRequestOptimized
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(2)
    runtimeState.cacheInvitationRemotes()
end)

runtimeState.findLocalPlayerPlot = function()
    if _lockedPlot then
        if _lockedPlot.Parent then
            return _lockedPlot
        end
    end

    _lockedPlot = nil

    local Plots = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

    if not Plots then
        warn("[getMyPlot] No existe workspace.Plots")

        return nil
    end

    local Character2 = LocalPlayer.Character
    local HumanoidRootPart = Character2 and Character2:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not HumanoidRootPart then
        warn("[getMyPlot] No se encontró HumanoidRootPart")

        return nil
    end

    local child32
    local huge = math.huge

    for _34, child9 in ipairs(Plots:GetChildren()) do
        local Spawn = child9:FindFirstChild("Spawn")

        if Spawn and Spawn:IsA("BasePart") then
            local Magnitude = (Spawn.Position - HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude

            if Magnitude < huge then
                huge = Magnitude
                child32 = child9
            end
        end
    end

    if child32 then
        _lockedPlot = child32
        print("[getMyPlot] Plot detectado:", child32.Name, "distancia:", math.floor(huge))
    end

    return child32
end
runtimeState.findTextInObject = function(object)
    if not object then
        return nil
    end

    if
        (object:IsA("TextLabel") or object:IsA("TextButton") or object:IsA("TextBox"))
        and object.Text ~= ""
    then
        return object.Text
    end

    for _35, descendant9 in ipairs(object:GetDescendants()) do
        if
            (
                descendant9:IsA("TextLabel")
                or descendant9:IsA("TextButton")
                or descendant9:IsA("TextBox")
            ) and descendant9.Text ~= ""
        then
            return descendant9.Text
        end
    end

    return nil
end
runtimeState.findFirstNamedDescendant = function(parent5, names)
    if not parent5 then
        return nil
    end

    for _36, value17 in ipairs(names) do
        local result59 = parent5:FindFirstChild(value17, true)

        if result59 then
            return result59
        end
    end

    return nil
end
runtimeState.readAnimalDisplayName = function(overhead)
    local firstNamedDescendant = runtimeState.findFirstNamedDescendant(overhead, {
        "DisplayName",
        "Name",
        "Title",
    })

    return runtimeState.findTextInObject(firstNamedDescendant) or "Desconocido"
end
runtimeState.readAnimalGeneration = function(overhead2)
    local firstNamedDescendant2 = runtimeState.findFirstNamedDescendant(overhead2, {
        "Generation",
        "Gen",
        "Cash",
        "Income",
    })

    return runtimeState.findTextInObject(firstNamedDescendant2) or "Desconocido"
end
runtimeState.extractAssetIdentifier = function(assetReference)
    if type(assetReference) == "string" then
        return assetReference:match("rbxassetid://(%d+)")
            or assetReference:match("[?&]id=(%d+)")
            or assetReference:match("(%d+)")
    end

    return nil
end

runtimeState.entries16 = {}

for name, data2 in pairs(runtimeState.ballSettings) do
    if type(data2) == "table" then
        local result60 = runtimeState.extractAssetIdentifier(data2.Icon)

        if result60 then
            runtimeState.entries16[result60] = {
                name = name,
                display = data2.Display or name,
                data = data2,
            }
        end
    end
end

runtimeState.buildTraitCatalog = function(traitDefinitions)
    local entries17 = {}
    local entries18 = {}
    local entries19 = {}

    if type(traitDefinitions) ~= "table" then
        return "None", entries18
    end

    for _37, value18 in ipairs(traitDefinitions) do
        local imageId

        if type(value18) == "table" then
            imageId = value18.imageId or value18.image or value18.Icon
        elseif type(value18) == "string" then
            imageId = value18
        end

        local imageId2 = runtimeState.extractAssetIdentifier(imageId)
        local imageId22 = imageId2 and runtimeState.entries16[imageId2]

        if not imageId22 or entries19[imageId22.name] then
            if imageId2 and not entries19["unknown_" .. imageId2] then
                entries19["unknown_" .. imageId2] = true
                table.insert(entries18, {
                    name = nil,
                    display = nil,
                    imageId = imageId2,
                    unknown = true,
                })
                warn("[resolveTraits] Trait no reconocido. Image ID:", imageId2)
            end
        else
            entries19[imageId22.name] = true
            table.insert(entries17, imageId22.display)
            table.insert(entries18, {
                name = imageId22.name,
                display = imageId22.display,
                imageId = imageId2,
                icon = imageId22.data.Icon,
                color = imageId22.data.Color,
            })
        end
    end

    if #entries17 == 0 then
        return "None", entries18
    end

    return table.concat(entries17, ", "), entries18
end
runtimeState.readAnimalTraits = function(overhead3)
    local Traits = overhead3:FindFirstChild("Traits", true)

    if not Traits then
        return {}
    end

    local entries20 = {}
    local entries21 = {}

    for _38, child10 in ipairs(Traits:GetChildren()) do
        if child10.Name == "Template" and child10:IsA("GuiObject") and child10.Visible then
            local object2

            if child10:IsA("ImageLabel") or child10:IsA("ImageButton") then
                object2 = child10
            else
                object2 = child10:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageLabel", true)
                    or child10:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ImageButton", true)
            end

            if object2 and object2.Visible then
                if object2.Image ~= "" then
                    local imageId3 = runtimeState.extractAssetIdentifier(object2.Image)

                    if imageId3 and not entries21[imageId3] then
                        entries21[imageId3] = true
                        table.insert(entries20, {
                            name = child10.Name,
                            imageId = imageId3,
                            image = object2.Image,
                            object = object2,
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return entries20
end
runtimeState.normalizeSearchText = function(text22)
    if type(text22) == "string" then
        return text22:lower():gsub("%s+", " "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    end

    return ""
end
runtimeState.parseGenerationAmount = function(generationText)
    if type(generationText) ~= "string" then
        return nil
    end

    local match4, result61 = generationText
        :gsub("%$", "")
        :gsub(",", "")
        :gsub("/s", "")
        :gsub("%s+", "")
        :upper()
        :match("([%d%.]+)([KMBTQ]?)")
    local numericValue2 = tonumber(match4)

    if numericValue2 then
        return numericValue2
            * (
                ({
                    K = 1000,
                    M = 1000000,
                    B = 1e9,
                    T = 1e12,
                    Q = 1e15,
                })[result61] or 1
            )
    end

    return nil
end
runtimeState.doesAnimalNameMatch = function(firstName, secondName, animalModel3)
    local result62 = runtimeState.normalizeSearchText(firstName)
    local result63 = runtimeState.normalizeSearchText(secondName)

    if result62 == result63 then
        return true
    end

    local animals = runtimeState.Animals and runtimeState.Animals[firstName]

    if animals then
        if
            runtimeState.normalizeSearchText(animals.DisplayName or animals.Name or firstName)
            == result63
        then
            return true
        end
    end

    if animalModel3 then
        if runtimeState.normalizeSearchText(animalModel3.Name) == result62 then
            return true
        end

        local Model = animalModel3:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")

        if Model and runtimeState.normalizeSearchText(Model.Name) == result62 then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end
runtimeState.isPointInsidePart = function(worldPosition, part)
    if typeof(worldPosition) ~= "Vector3" then
        return false
    end

    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
        return false
    end

    local result64 = part.CFrame:PointToObjectSpace(worldPosition)
    local scaledValue2 = part.Size * 0.5

    return math.abs(result64.X) <= scaledValue2.X
        and math.abs(result64.Y) <= scaledValue2.Y
        and math.abs(result64.Z) <= scaledValue2.Z
end
runtimeState.readAnimalMutation = function(overhead4)
    local firstNamedDescendant3 = runtimeState.findFirstNamedDescendant(overhead4, {
        "Mutation",
        "Mutations",
        "MutationLabel",
    })

    return runtimeState.findTextInObject(firstNamedDescendant3) or "None"
end
runtimeState.collectAnimalListingData = function(animalName3)
    local localPlayerPlot = runtimeState.findLocalPlayerPlot()

    if not localPlayerPlot then
        warn("[getAnimalFullData] No se encontró el plot")

        return nil
    end

    local StealHitbox = localPlayerPlot:FindFirstChild("StealHitbox", true)

    if not StealHitbox or not StealHitbox:IsA("BasePart") then
        warn("[getAnimalFullData] No existe StealHitbox en:", localPlayerPlot:GetFullName())

        return nil
    end

    local Debris = workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")

    if not Debris then
        warn("[getAnimalFullData] No existe workspace.Debris")

        return nil
    end

    for _39, child11 in ipairs(Debris:GetChildren()) do
        if child11.Name ~= "FastOverheadTemplate" then
            continue
        end

        local SurfaceGui = child11:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("SurfaceGui", true)
        local Adornee = SurfaceGui and SurfaceGui.Adornee

        if
            not SurfaceGui
            or not Adornee
            or not Adornee:IsA("BasePart")
            or not runtimeState.isPointInsidePart(Adornee.Position, StealHitbox)
        then
            continue
        end

        local name2 = runtimeState.readAnimalDisplayName(SurfaceGui)
        local result65 = runtimeState.readAnimalMutation(SurfaceGui)
        local genText2 = runtimeState.readAnimalGeneration(SurfaceGui)

        if name2 ~= "Desconocido" and genText2 ~= "Desconocido" and result65 ~= "Desconocido" then
            if not runtimeState.doesAnimalNameMatch(animalName3, name2, Adornee) then
                continue
            end

            local result66 = runtimeState.readAnimalTraits(SurfaceGui)
            local traits3, traitsData = runtimeState.buildTraitCatalog(result66)
            local animals2 = runtimeState.Animals and runtimeState.Animals[animalName3]

            return {
                name = name2,
                modelName = animalName3,
                rarity = animals2 and animals2.Rarity or "Unknown",
                genValue = runtimeState.parseGenerationAmount(genText2),
                genText = genText2,
                mutation = result65 ~= "" and result65 or "None",
                traits = traits3,
                traitsData = traitsData,
                slot = Adornee.Name,
                adornee = Adornee,
                position = Adornee.Position,
                template = child11,
            }
        end
    end

    return nil
end

sendAnimalToPvPAPI = function(animalName4)
    if runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(LocalPlayer.Name) then
        runtimeState.rememberPremiumListing(LocalPlayer.Name)
    end

    local result67 = runtimeState.collectAnimalListingData(animalName4)

    if not runtimeState.isSocketConnected then
        showNotification("❌ WebSocket Error", "Sin conexión al servidor", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    local data3 = {
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animal = animalName4,
        animalDisplayName = result67 and result67.name or animalName4,
        rarity = result67 and result67.rarity or "Unknown",
        genValue = result67 and result67.genValue or 0,
        genText = result67 and result67.genText or "$0/s",
        mutation = result67 and result67.mutation or "None",
        traits = result67 and result67.traits or "None",
    }

    data3.timestamp = os.time()

    local idSettings = {
        id = "pending_" .. tostring(os.time()) .. "_" .. tostring(math.random(10000, 99999)),
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animal = animalName4,
        animalDisplayName = result67 and result67.name or animalName4,
        rarity = result67 and result67.rarity or "Unknown",
    }
    local genText = result67 and result67.genText

    idSettings.genText = genText or "$0/s"
    idSettings.mutation = result67 and result67.mutation or "None"
    idSettings.traits = result67 and result67.traits or "None"
    idSettings.timestamp = os.time()
    table.insert(runtimeState.pvpListings, 1, idSettings)

    if #runtimeState.pvpListings > 24 then
        table.remove(runtimeState.pvpListings)
    end

    if
        not runtimeState.isInterfacePaused
        and runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen
        and runtimeState.parent11
    then
        updatePlayersListIncremental()
    end

    local Send8 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

    if not Send8 then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "WebSocket no soporta envío", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    local json6 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
        type = "new_duel",
        data = data3,
    })
    local ok12, result68 = pcall(function()
        Send8(runtimeState.socketConnection, json6)
    end)

    if not ok12 then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "Falló al enviar: " .. tostring(result68), 3, true)

        return false
    end

    showNotification("✅ Duelo Publicado", string.format("%s añadido a la lista", animalName4), 2)

    return true
end

sendGearToPvPAPI = function(gearName)

        return false
    end

    local data7 = {
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animal = gearName,
        animalDisplayName = " " .. gearName .. " ",
        rarity = " ",
        genText = " ",
        mutation = "None",
        traits = "None",
        type = "gear",
        timestamp = os.time(),
    }

    table.insert(runtimeState.pvpListings, 1, {
        id = "pending_gear_" .. tostring(os.time()) .. "_" .. tostring(math.random(10000, 99999)),
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animal = gearName,
        animalDisplayName = " " .. gearName .. " ",
        rarity = " ",
        genText = " ",
        mutation = "None",
        traits = "None",
        type = "gear",
        timestamp = os.time(),
    })

    if #runtimeState.pvpListings > 24 then
        table.remove(runtimeState.pvpListings)
    end

    if
        not runtimeState.isInterfacePaused
        and runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen
        and runtimeState.parent11
    then
        updatePlayersListIncremental()
    end

    local Send12 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

    if not Send12 then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "WebSocket no soporta envío", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    local json10 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
        type = "new_duel",
        data = data7,
    })
    local ok16, result72 = pcall(function()
        Send12(runtimeState.socketConnection, json10)
    end)

    if ok16 then
        showNotification(
            "✅ Gear Publicado",
            string.format("%s añadido a la lista PvP", gearName),
            2
        )

        return true
    end

    showNotification("❌ Error", "Falló al enviar: " .. tostring(result72), 3, true)

    return false
end
sendGearToTradeAPI = function(gearName2)
    if runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(LocalPlayer.Name) then
        runtimeState.rememberPremiumListing(LocalPlayer.Name)
    end

    if not runtimeState.isSocketConnected then
        showNotification("❌ WebSocket Error", "Sin conexión al servidor", 3, true)

        return false
    end

    local id3 = "local_gear_" .. tostring(os.time()) .. "_" .. tostring(math.random(10000, 99999))

    runtimeState.cachedAnimalAssets[id3] = true

    local data8 = {
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animal = gearName2,
        status = "looking",
        animalDisplayName = " " .. gearName2 .. " ",
        rarity = "Gear",
        genText = "",
        mutation = "None",
        traits = "None",
        type = "gear",
        timestamp = os.time(),
    }

    table.insert(runtimeState.tradeListings, 1, {
        id = id3,
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animal = gearName2,
        status = "looking",
        animalDisplayName = " " .. gearName2 .. " ",
        rarity = "Gear",
        genText = " Gear",
        mutation = "None",
        traits = "None",
        type = "gear",
        timestamp = os.time(),
    })

    if #runtimeState.tradeListings > 24 then
        table.remove(runtimeState.tradeListings)
    end

    if
        not runtimeState.isInterfacePaused
        and runtimeState.isTradeFinderOpen
        and runtimeState.parent12
    then
        updateTradePlayersListIncremental()
    end

    local Send13 = runtimeState.socketConnection.Send or runtimeState.socketConnection.send

    if not Send13 then
        showNotification("❌ Error", "WebSocket no soporta envío", 3, true)
        runtimeState.cachedAnimalAssets[id3] = nil

        return false
    end

    local json11 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
        type = "new_trade",
        data = data8,
    })
    local ok17, result73 = pcall(function()
        Send13(runtimeState.socketConnection, json11)
    end)

    if ok17 then
        runtimeState.cachedAnimalAssets[id3] = nil
        showNotification(
            "✅ Gear Publicado",
            string.format("%s añadido a la lista de Trades", gearName2),
            2
        )

        return true
    end

    showNotification("❌ Error", "Falló al enviar: " .. tostring(result73), 3, true)
    runtimeState.cachedAnimalAssets[id3] = nil

    return false
end

runtimeState.collectVisiblePlotAnimals = function(plot)
    local Debris2 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Debris")

    if not Debris2 then
        return {}
    end

    local entries22 = {}

    for _40, child12 in pairs(Debris2:GetChildren()) do
        if child12.Name == "FastOverheadTemplate" then
            local AnimalOverhead = child12:FindFirstChild("AnimalOverhead")

            if AnimalOverhead then
                local Generation = AnimalOverhead:FindFirstChild("Generation")
                local DisplayName = AnimalOverhead:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")

                if DisplayName and DisplayName:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    local Text = DisplayName.Text or ""
                    local result692 = string.gsub(string.lower(Text), "%s+", "")
                    local result702 = string.gsub(string.lower(plot), "%s+", "")

                    if string.find(result692, result702) or string.find(result702, result692) then
                        local generation = ""

                        if Generation then
                            if Generation:IsA("TextLabel") then
                                generation = Generation.Text or ""
                            end
                        end

                        table.insert(entries22, {
                            Generation = generation,
                            DisplayName = Text,
                            OriginalName = plot,
                        })
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return entries22
end

    local function createFrame32(value54, parent71, layoutOrder10)
        if not value54:IsA("Model") then
            return
        end

        local parent72 = Instance.new("Frame")

        parent72.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        parent72.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder10
        parent72.Parent = parent71

        local parent73 = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")

        parent73.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.75, 0)
        parent73.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        parent73.BorderSizePixel = 0
        parent73.Parent = parent72

        local addButton = Instance.new("TextButton")

        addButton.Name = "AddButton"
        addButton.Size = UDim2.new(0.8, 0, 0.18, 0)
        addButton.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.88, 0)
        addButton.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        addButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

        local result97, result98, result99 = runtimeState.canPublishListing()

        if result97 then
            addButton.Text = onSelected3 and "📦 OFFER" or "⚔️ ADD"
        else
            addButton.Text = string.format("⏳ %d:%02d", result98, result99)
        end

        addButton.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        addButton.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        addButton.TextScaled = true
        addButton.Parent = parent72

        local uiCorner40 = Instance.new("UICorner")

        uiCorner40.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
        uiCorner40.Parent = addButton

        local parent74 = Instance.new("WorldModel")

        parent74.Parent = parent73

        local currentCamera3 = Instance.new("Camera")

        currentCamera3.FieldOfView = 70
        currentCamera3.Parent = parent73
        parent73.CurrentCamera = currentCamera3

        local clone4 = value54:Clone()

        clone4:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 0, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(180), 0))
        clone4.Parent = parent74

        local Humanoid3 = value54:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            or value54:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")
        local Humanoid22 = clone4:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            or clone4:FindFirstChildOfClass("AnimationController")

        if Humanoid3 and Humanoid22 then
            task.spawn(function()
                local Animator2 = Humanoid22:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                    or Instance.new("Animator", Humanoid22)

                RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()

                for _45, value21 in pairs(Humanoid3:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    local result100 = Animator2:LoadAnimation(value21.Animation)

                    result100.Looped = true
                    result100:Play()
                    result100.TimePosition = value21.TimePosition
                end
            end)
        end

        local _46, result101 = clone4:GetBoundingBox()

        currentCamera3.CFrame = CFrame.new(
            Vector3.new(0, result101.Y / 2, math.max(result101.X, result101.Y, result101.Z) * 1.3),
            Vector3.new(0, result101.Y / 2, 0)
        )

        local textButton3 = Instance.new("TextButton")

        textButton3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        textButton3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        textButton3.Text = ""
        textButton3.Parent = parent73

        local tweenInfo3 = TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
        local currentCamera32
        local tween2
        local addButton2

        textButton3.Activated:Connect(function()
            if currentCamera32 == currentCamera3 then
                TweenService:Create(currentCamera3, tweenInfo3, {
                    FieldOfView = 70,
                }):Play()
                addButton.Visible = false
                currentCamera32 = nil
            else
                if currentCamera32 then
                    tween2:Play()

                    if addButton2 then
                        addButton2.Visible = false
                    end
                end

                TweenService:Create(currentCamera3, tweenInfo3, {
                    FieldOfView = 45,
                }):Play()
                addButton.Visible = true
                currentCamera32 = currentCamera3
                tween2 = TweenService:Create(currentCamera3, tweenInfo3, {
                    FieldOfView = 70,
                })
                addButton2 = addButton
            end
        end)
        task.spawn(function()
            while addButton and addButton.Parent do
                local result102, result103, result104 = runtimeState.canPublishListing()

                if not result102 then
                    addButton.Text = string.format("⏳ %d:%02d", result103, result104)
                    addButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 193, 7)
                elseif addButton.Text ~= (onSelected3 and "📦 OFFER" or "⚔️ ADD") then
                    addButton.Text = onSelected3 and "📦 OFFER" or "⚔️ ADD"
                    addButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                end

                task.wait(1)
            end
        end)
        addButton.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local Name25 = value54.Name
            local result105, result106, result107 = runtimeState.canPublishListing()

            if not result105 then
                showNotification(
                    "⏳ Cooldown Global",
                    string.format(
                        "Espera %d:%02d antes de añadir otro animal",
                        result106,
                        result107
                    ),
                    3
                )

                return
            end

            local result108 = runtimeState.collectVisiblePlotAnimals(Name25)

            if #result108 > 0 then
                addButton.Text = onSelected3 and "✅ OFFERED!" or "✅ ADDED!"
                addButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 167, 69)
                showNotification("✅ Animal Añadido", "Añadido: " .. result108[1].DisplayName, 2)
            else
                addButton.Text = onSelected3 and "✅ OFFERED!" or "✅ ADDED!"
                addButton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 193, 7)
                showNotification("⚠️ Animal Añadido", "Añadido: " .. Name25, 2)
            end

            runtimeState.startListingCooldown()
            task.spawn(function()
                if onSelected3 then
                    sendAnimalToTradeAPI(Name25)
                else
                    sendAnimalToPvPAPI(Name25)
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)

            if plotInventoryGUI and plotInventoryGUI.Parent then
                plotInventoryGUI:Destroy()
            end

            task.wait(0.5)

            if onSelected3 then
                openTradeFinder()
            else
                openPvPFinder()
            end
        end)

        return parent72
    end

    if playerPlot3 then

                createFrame32(child14, parent62, counter8)
                counter8 += 1
                counter9 += 1

        end

        if counter9 == 0 and #playerOwnedSkinsFromGUI == 0 and #result109 == 0 then

            textLabel13.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)

            textLabel13.TextSize = 14
            textLabel13.BackgroundTransparency = 1

        end
    elseif #playerOwnedSkinsFromGUI == 0 and #result109 == 0 then
        local noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)

    end

    parent75.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 10)
    parent75.Text = "X"

                    mutation = result112.mutation,
                    traits = result112.traits,
                }

                task.wait(0.3)
                runtimeState.joinMatchmaking(Name26, displayNameSettings)

                if matchmakingAnimalSelection and matchmakingAnimalSelection.Parent then
                    matchmakingAnimalSelection:Destroy()
                end

                task.wait(0.3)
                openPvPFinder()
            else
                showNotification("❌ Error", "No se pudieron obtener datos del animal", 3, true)
            end
        end)

        return parent79
    end

    local counter10 = 1
    local playerPlot4 = findPlayerPlot(LocalPlayer.Name)
    local friendPanelSettings4 = {
        FriendPanel = true,
        CashPad = true,
        Cash = true,
        PlotSign = true,
        Plotsign = true,
        Model = true,
    }

    if playerPlot4 then
        local counter11 = 0

        for _52, child15 in ipairs(playerPlot4:GetChildren()) do
            if child15:IsA("Model") and not friendPanelSettings4[child15.Name] then
                createFrame5(child15, parent77, counter10)
                counter10 += 1
                counter11 += 1
            end
        end

        if counter11 == 0 then
            local textLabel16 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

            textLabel16.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
            textLabel16.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -25)
            textLabel16.Text = "No se encontraron animales en tu plot"
            textLabel16.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            textLabel16.TextSize = 14
            textLabel16.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            textLabel16.ZIndex = 5002
            textLabel16.Parent = parent77
        end
    else
        local noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 50)
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -25)
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.Text = "No se encontró tu parcela"
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.TextSize = 14
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.ZIndex = 5002
        noSeEncontrTuParcelaLabel3.Parent = parent77
    end

    local parent82 = Instance.new("TextButton")

    parent82.Size = UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40)
    parent82.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, 10)
    parent82.Text = "X"
    parent82.TextColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    parent82.TextSize = 20
    parent82.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    parent82.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 53, 69)
    parent82.BorderSizePixel = 0
    parent82.ZIndex = 5002
    parent82.Parent = parent76

    local uiCorner43 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner43.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    uiCorner43.Parent = parent82
    parent82.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if matchmakingAnimalSelection and matchmakingAnimalSelection.Parent then
            matchmakingAnimalSelection:Destroy()
        end

        task.wait(0.2)
        openPvPFinder()
    end)
end
findPlayerPlot = function(value56)
    local Plots2 = workspace:FindFirstChild("Plots")

    if not Plots2 then
        return nil
    end

    for _53, child16 in pairs(Plots2:GetChildren()) do
        local PlotSign = child16:FindFirstChild("PlotSign")

        if not PlotSign then
            continue
        end

        local YourBase = PlotSign:FindFirstChild("YourBase")

        if not YourBase or not YourBase:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            continue
        end

        if YourBase.Enabled ~= true then
            continue
        end

        local textElement2 = PlotSign:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)

        if textElement2 and string.find(string.lower(textElement2.Text), string.lower(value56)) then
            return child16
        end

        return child16
    end

    return nil
end
getPlayersFromAPI = function()
    local ok19, result113 = pcall(function()
        return request({
            Url = Duels,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = {
                ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                ["Cache-Control"] = "no-cache",
            },
        })
    end)

    if ok19 and result113 and result113.StatusCode == 200 then
        local ok22, result210 = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(result113.Body)
        end)

        if ok22 and result210 then
            local entries24 = {}
            local entries25 = {}

            table.sort(result210, function(value57, value58)
                return (tonumber(value57.timestamp) or 0) > (tonumber(value58.timestamp) or 0)
            end)

            for _54, value25 in ipairs(result210) do
                local textValue2 = tostring(value25.userId or "")
                local tostring_ = tostring
                local animal2 = value25.animal
                local formattedText4 = textValue2 .. "_" .. tostring_(animal2 or "")

                if not entries25[formattedText4] then
                    entries25[formattedText4] = true
                    table.insert(entries24, {
                        id = value25.id,
                        userId = tonumber(value25.userId),
                        username = value25.username,
                        animal = value25.animal,
                        animalDisplayName = value25.animalDisplayName or value25.animal,
                        rarity = value25.rarity or "Unknown",
                        genText = value25.genText or "$0/s",
                        mutation = value25.mutation or "None",
                        traits = value25.traits or "None",
                        timestamp = tonumber(value25.timestamp) or 0,
                        status = value25.status,
                    })
                end
            end

            if filterData.enabled and #filterData.whitelist > 0 then
                local entries26 = {}

                for _55, value26 in ipairs(entries24) do
                    if isAnimalInWhitelist(value26.animal) then
                        table.insert(entries26, value26)
                    end
                end

                entries24 = entries26
            end

            return runtimeState.mergePremiumListings(entries24)
        end
    end

    return {}
end
getTradePlayersFromAPI = function()
    local ok20, result114 = pcall(function()
        return request({
            Url = Trades,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = {
                ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                ["Cache-Control"] = "no-cache",
            },
        })
    end)

    if ok20 and result114 and result114.StatusCode == 200 then
        local ok23, result211 = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(result114.Body)
        end)

        if ok23 and result211 then
            local entries27 = {}
            local entries28 = {}

            table.sort(result211, function(value59, value60)
                return (tonumber(value59.timestamp) or 0) > (tonumber(value60.timestamp) or 0)
            end)

            for _56, value27 in ipairs(result211) do
                local formattedText5 = tostring(value27.userId or "")
                    .. "_"
                    .. tostring(value27.animal or "")

                if not entries28[formattedText5] then
                    entries28[formattedText5] = true
                    table.insert(entries27, {
                        id = value27.id,
                        userId = tonumber(value27.userId),
                        username = value27.username,
                        animal = value27.animal,
                        animalDisplayName = value27.animalDisplayName or value27.animal,
                        rarity = value27.rarity or "Unknown",
                        genText = value27.genText or "$0/s",
                        mutation = value27.mutation or "None",
                        traits = value27.traits or "None",
                        timestamp = tonumber(value27.timestamp) or 0,
                        status = value27.status or "looking",
                    })
                end
            end

            if filterData.enabled and #filterData.whitelist > 0 then
                local entries29 = {}

                for _57, value28 in ipairs(entries27) do
                    if isAnimalInWhitelist(value28.animal) then
                        table.insert(entries29, value28)
                    end
                end

                entries27 = entries29
            end

            return runtimeState.mergePremiumListings(entries27)
        end
    end

    return {}
end

runtimeState.connectSocket = function()
    if not WebSocket then
        error("❌ WebSocket no disponible")

        return false
    end

    if runtimeState.socketConnection then
        pcall(function()
            if runtimeState.socketConnection.Close then
                runtimeState.socketConnection:Close()
            elseif runtimeState.socketConnection.close then
                runtimeState.socketConnection:close()
            end
        end)
        runtimeState.socketConnection = nil
    end

    print("🟡 Conectando a WebSocket...")
    updateConnectionStatus(false, "Conectando...", false)

    local ok21, result115 = pcall(function()
        return WebSocket.connect("wss://zlhub.net/ws")
    end)

    if not ok21 or not result115 then
        updateConnectionStatus(false, "Error de conexión", false)
        error("❌ Error conectando a WebSocket")

        return false
    end

    runtimeState.socketConnection = result115
    runtimeState.isSocketConnected = true
    runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = false
    print("✅ Conectado a WebSocket, iniciando handshake...")
    updateConnectionStatus(false, "Autenticando...", false)

    if not runtimeState.authenticateSocket() then
        print("❌ Handshake falló, cerrando conexión")
        updateConnectionStatus(false, "Handshake falló", true)
        showNotification(
            "❌ Autenticación Falló",
            "No se pudo autenticar con el servidor",
            5,
            true
        )
        pcall(function()
            if runtimeState.socketConnection.Close then
                runtimeState.socketConnection:Close()
            elseif runtimeState.socketConnection.close then
                runtimeState.socketConnection:close()
            end
        end)
        runtimeState.isSocketConnected = false
        runtimeState.socketConnection = nil

        return false
    end

    updateConnectionStatus(true)
    task.wait(0.5)

    local json12 = HttpService:JSONEncode({
        type = "subscribe",
        channels = {
            "duels",
            "trades",
        },
    })

    pcall(function()
        if runtimeState.socketConnection.Send then
            runtimeState.socketConnection:Send(json12)
        elseif runtimeState.socketConnection.send then
            runtimeState.socketConnection:send(json12)
        end
    end)

    if runtimeState.socketConnection.OnMessage then
        runtimeState.socketConnection.OnMessage:Connect(function(value61)
            local result1132 = value61:gsub("[%z\1-\31]", ""):gsub("^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")
            local ok24, result213 = pcall(function()
                return HttpService:JSONDecode(result1132)
            end)

            if not ok24 or not result213 then
                return
            end

            if result213.type == "handshake_response" then
                if result213.success then
                    runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = true
                    updateConnectionStatus(true)
                else
                    print("❌ [WS] Handshake falló:", result213.error)
                    runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = false
                    updateConnectionStatus(
                        false,
                        "Handshake falló: " .. (result213.error or ""),
                        true
                    )
                end

                return
            end

            if result213.type == "subscribed" then
                return
            end

            if result213.type == "private_message" then
                local isMatching2 = result213.fromUserId == LocalPlayer.UserId
                local toUserId = isMatching2 and result213.toUserId or result213.fromUserId
                local Name27 = isMatching2 and LocalPlayer.Name or result213.fromUsername

                if result213.toUserId == LocalPlayer.UserId or isMatching2 then
                    if result213.isCard and result213.animalData then
                        runtimeState.appendChatMessage(
                            toUserId,
                            Name27,
                            "",
                            isMatching2,
                            true,
                            result213.animalData
                        )
                    else
                        runtimeState.appendChatMessage(
                            toUserId,
                            Name27,
                            result213.text or "",
                            isMatching2
                        )
                    end

                    if not isMatching2 then
                        showNotification(
                            "💬 " .. Name27,
                            result213.text or "🎴 Te envió un animal",
                            5
                        )
                        updateChatNotificationsUI()
                    end

                    if runtimeState.entries9[toUserId] then
                    end
                end

                return
            end

            if result213.type == "matchmaking_status" then
                if result213.status == "searching" then
                    runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = true
                    runtimeState.matchmakingQueueSize = result213.queueSize or 0
                    runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()
                elseif result213.status == "already_in_queue" then
                    runtimeState.matchmakingQueueSize = result213.queueSize or 0
                    runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()
                elseif result213.status == "left_queue" then
                    runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = false
                    runtimeState.matchmakingQueueSize = 0
                    runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()
                end

                return
            end

            if result213.type == "matchmaking_timeout" then
                runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = false
                runtimeState.matchmakingQueueSize = 0
                runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()
                showNotification("⏰ Timeout", result213.message or "No se encontró oponente", 3)

                return
            end

            if result213.type == "match_found" then
                runtimeState.isMatchmakingActive = false
                runtimeState.matchmakingQueueSize = 0
                runtimeState.updateMatchmakingButton()

                local animal3 = result213.animal or "?"
                local animal22 = result213.opponent and result213.opponent.animal or "?"

                if result213.action == "invite" then
                    local opponent = result213.opponent

                    showNotification(
                        "🎯 ¡Match Encontrado!",
                        "🎮 ["
                            .. animal3
                            .. "] vs "
                            .. opponent.username
                            .. " ["
                            .. animal22
                            .. "]",
                        4
                    )
                    task.spawn(function()
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        runtimeState.sendDuelInvitationOptimized(opponent.userId, opponent.username)
                    end)
                elseif result213.action == "wait" then
                    showNotification(
                        "🎯 ¡Match Encontrado!",
                        "🎮 "
                            .. result213.opponent.username
                            .. " ["
                            .. animal22
                            .. "] te invitará",
                        4
                    )
                end

                return
            end

            if result213.type == "new_duel" and result213.data then
                if filterData then
                    if
                        filterData.enabled
                        and #filterData.whitelist > 0
                        and not isAnimalInWhitelist(result213.data.animal)
                    then
                        return
                    end
                end

                if runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(result213.data.username) then
                    runtimeState.rememberPremiumListing(result213.data.username)
                end

                local isEnabled11 = false

                for _58, value29 in ipairs(runtimeState.pvpListings) do
                    if value29.id == result213.data.id then
                        isEnabled11 = true

                        break
                    end
                end

                if not isEnabled11 then
                    table.insert(runtimeState.pvpListings, 1, result213.data)

                    while #runtimeState.pvpListings > 24 do
                        table.remove(runtimeState.pvpListings)
                    end

                    runtimeState.pvpListings =
                        runtimeState.mergePremiumListings(runtimeState.pvpListings)

                    if runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen and runtimeState.parent11 then
                        updatePlayersListIncremental()
                    end
                end

                return
            end

            if result213.type ~= "new_trade" or not result213.data then
                if result213.type ~= "error" then
                    return
                end

                print("❌ [WS] Error del servidor:", result213.error)
                showNotification("❌ Error", result213.error, 5, true)

                return
            end

            if
                filterData
                and filterData.enabled
                and #filterData.whitelist > 0
                and not isAnimalInWhitelist(result213.data.animal)
            then
                return
            end

            if runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(result213.data.username) then
                runtimeState.rememberPremiumListing(result213.data.username)
            end

            local isEnabled12 = false

            for _59, value30 in ipairs(runtimeState.tradeListings) do
                if value30.id == result213.data.id then
                    isEnabled12 = true

                    break
                end
            end

            if not isEnabled12 then
                table.insert(runtimeState.tradeListings, 1, result213.data)

                while #runtimeState.tradeListings > 24 do
                    table.remove(runtimeState.tradeListings)
                end

                runtimeState.tradeListings =
                    runtimeState.mergePremiumListings(runtimeState.tradeListings)

                if runtimeState.isTradeFinderOpen and runtimeState.parent12 then
                    updateTradePlayersListIncremental()
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    if runtimeState.socketConnection.OnClose then
        runtimeState.socketConnection.OnClose:Connect(function()
            print("⚠️ WebSocket cerrado")
            runtimeState.socketConnection = nil
            runtimeState.isSocketConnected = false
            runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = false
            task.spawn(function()
                task.wait(2)
                attemptReconnect()
            end)
        end)
    end

    if runtimeState.socketConnection.OnError then
        runtimeState.socketConnection.OnError:Connect(function(value62)
            print("❌ WebSocket error:", value62)
            runtimeState.socketConnection = nil
            runtimeState.isSocketConnected = false
            runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = false
        end)
    end

    return true
end

runtimeState.createFrame6 = function(listing2, layoutOrder3, listingType)
    local parent87 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent87.Name = "PlayerCard_" .. (listing2.id or layoutOrder3)
    parent87.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 80)
    parent87.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 40)
    parent87.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
    parent87.BorderSizePixel = 0
    parent87.Parent = nil

    local uiCorner49 = Instance.new("UICorner")

    uiCorner49.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    uiCorner49.Parent = parent87

    if runtimeState.isPremiumUsername(listing2.username) then
        local parent88 = Instance.new("UIStroke")

        parent88.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)

            local rotation = 0

            while parent87 and parent87.Parent do
                rotation = (rotation + 5) % 360
                uiGradient.Rotation = rotation
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)
    end

    local infoContainer = Instance.new("Frame")

    infoContainer.Name = "InfoContainer"
    infoContainer.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 1, -10)
    infoContainer.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)

    uiListLayout13.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    uiListLayout13.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Left
    uiListLayout13.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    uiListLayout13.Padding = UDim.new(0, 10)
    uiListLayout13.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
    uiListLayout13.Parent = infoContainer

    local parent89 = Instance.new("Frame")

    parent89.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 1, 0)
    parent89.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    parent89.LayoutOrder = 1
    parent89.Parent = infoContainer

    local animalDisplayName = listing2.type == "skin"
        or listing2.rarity == "Skin"
        or listing2.animalDisplayName and string.find(listing2.animalDisplayName, "(Skin)")
    local animalDisplayName2 = listing2.type == "gear"
        or listing2.rarity == "Gear"
        or listing2.animalDisplayName and string.find(listing2.animalDisplayName, "(Gear)")
    local aquaticSettings3 = {
        Aquatic = "rbxassetid://100987404805977",
