updatePlayersListIncremental = function()
    if runtimeState.isInterfacePaused then
        return
    end

    local PlayersScrollingFrame = runtimeState.parent11
        and runtimeState.parent11:FindFirstChild("PlayersScrollingFrame")

    if not PlayersScrollingFrame then
        return
    end

    local pvpListings2 = runtimeState.pvpListings or {}
    local entries31 = {}

    for _62, child17 in ipairs(PlayersScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child17:IsA("Frame") then
            if not child17.Name:match("^PlayerCard_") then
                if not child17.Name:match("^Card_") then
                    continue
                end
            end

            local attribute8 = child17:GetAttribute("ItemId")

            if attribute8 then
                entries31[attribute8] = child17
            else
                child17:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    for i12, value31 in ipairs(pvpListings2) do
        if entries31[value31.id] then
            entries31[value31.id].LayoutOrder = i12
        else
            local frame2 = runtimeState.createFrame6(value31, i12, false)

            frame2:SetAttribute("ItemId", value31.id)
            frame2.Name = "PlayerCard_" .. value31.id
            frame2.LayoutOrder = i12
            frame2.Parent = PlayersScrollingFrame
        end
    end

    for key4, value32 in pairs(entries31) do
        local isEnabled13 = false

        for _63, value33 in ipairs(pvpListings2) do
            if value33.id == key4 then
                isEnabled13 = true

                break
            end
        end

        if not isEnabled13 then
            value32:Destroy()
        end
    end

    local entries32 = {}

    for _64, child18 in ipairs(PlayersScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child18:IsA("Frame") and child18:GetAttribute("ItemId") then
            table.insert(entries32, child18)
        end
    end

    table.sort(entries32, function(value63, value64)
        local LayoutOrder = value63.LayoutOrder or 999

        return LayoutOrder < (value64.LayoutOrder or 999)
    end)

    for i13, value34 in ipairs(entries32) do
        value34.LayoutOrder = i13
    end

    local combinedValue2 = 0

    for _65, child19 in ipairs(PlayersScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child19:IsA("Frame") and child19:GetAttribute("ItemId") then
            combinedValue2 = combinedValue2 + child19.AbsoluteSize.Y + 8
        end
    end

    PlayersScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(combinedValue2, 50))

    local EmptyMessage = PlayersScrollingFrame:FindFirstChild("EmptyMessage")

    if EmptyMessage then
        EmptyMessage:Destroy()
    end

    if #pvpListings2 == 0 then
        local emptyMessage = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        emptyMessage.Name = "EmptyMessage"
        emptyMessage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        emptyMessage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -20)
        emptyMessage.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        emptyMessage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        emptyMessage.Text = "No hay jugadores disponibles"
        emptyMessage.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        emptyMessage.TextSize = 13
        emptyMessage.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        emptyMessage.Parent = PlayersScrollingFrame
    end
end
updateTradePlayersListIncremental = function()
    if runtimeState.isInterfacePaused then
        return
    end

    local TradePlayersScrollingFrame = runtimeState.parent12
        and runtimeState.parent12:FindFirstChild("TradePlayersScrollingFrame")

    if not TradePlayersScrollingFrame then
        return
    end

    local tradeListings2 = runtimeState.tradeListings or {}
    local entries33 = {}

    for _66, child20 in ipairs(TradePlayersScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child20:IsA("Frame") then
            if not child20.Name:match("^TradeCard_") then
                if not child20.Name:match("^Card_") then
                    continue
                end
            end

            local attribute9 = child20:GetAttribute("ItemId")

            if attribute9 then
                entries33[attribute9] = child20
            else
                child20:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    for i14, value35 in ipairs(tradeListings2) do
        if i14 <= 24 then
            if entries33[value35.id] then
                entries33[value35.id].LayoutOrder = i14
            else
                local frame3 = runtimeState.createFrame6(value35, i14, true)

                frame3:SetAttribute("ItemId", value35.id)
                frame3.Name = "TradeCard_" .. value35.id
                frame3.LayoutOrder = i14
                frame3.Parent = TradePlayersScrollingFrame
            end
        end
    end

    local entries34 = {}

    for _67, child21 in ipairs(TradePlayersScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child21:IsA("Frame") then
            if not child21.Name:match("^TradeCard_") then
                if not child21.Name:match("^Card_") then
                    continue
                end
            end

            local attribute10 = child21:GetAttribute("ItemId")

            if attribute10 then
                local isEnabled14 = false

                for _68, value36 in ipairs(tradeListings2) do
                    if value36.id == attribute10 then
                        isEnabled14 = true

                        break
                    end
                end

                if isEnabled14 then
                    table.insert(entries34, child21)
                else
                    child21:Destroy()
                end
            else
                child21:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(entries34, function(value65, value66)
        return (value65.LayoutOrder or 999) < (value66.LayoutOrder or 999)
    end)

    for i15, value37 in ipairs(entries34) do
        value37.LayoutOrder = i15
    end

    if #entries34 > 24 then
        for i16 = 25, #entries34 do
            entries34[i16]:Destroy()
        end
    end

    local combinedValue3 = 0

    for _69, child23 in ipairs(TradePlayersScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
        if child23:IsA("Frame") then
            if not child23.Name:match("^TradeCard_") then
                if not child23.Name:match("^Card_") then
                    continue
                end
            end

            combinedValue3 = combinedValue3 + child23.AbsoluteSize.Y + 8
        end
    end

    TradePlayersScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(combinedValue3, 50))

    local EmptyMessage2 = TradePlayersScrollingFrame:FindFirstChild("EmptyMessage")

    if EmptyMessage2 then
        EmptyMessage2:Destroy()
    end

    if #entries34 == 0 then
        local emptyMessage2 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        emptyMessage2.Name = "EmptyMessage"
        emptyMessage2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        if runtimeState.isMainMenuOpen then
            fetchMessages()
            task.wait(0.8)
        else
            task.wait(5)
        end
    end
end)

UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input6, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then
        return
    end

    if input6.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F8 then
        toggleChat()
    elseif input6.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F9 then
        openPvPFinder()
    elseif input6.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.F10 then
        openTradeFinder()
    elseif input6.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Slash and runtimeState.isMainMenuOpen then
        input6:CaptureFocus()
    end
end)

filterData = {
    whitelist = {},
    enabled = false,
    searchText = "",
}

runtimeState.saveFilterWhitelist = function()
    local _70 = pcall(function()
        if not writefile then
            return
        end

        local json13 = HttpService:JSONEncode(filterData.whitelist)

        writefile("ZLChat_Filter_Whitelist.txt", json13)
    end)
end
runtimeState.loadFilterWhitelist = function()
    local ok25 = pcall(function()
        if not readfile then
            return
        end

        if isfile("ZLChat_Filter_Whitelist.txt") then
            local txt = readfile("ZLChat_Filter_Whitelist.txt")
            local data9 = HttpService:JSONDecode(txt)

            if type(data9) == "table" then
                filterData.whitelist = data9
            end
        end
    end)

    if not ok25 then
        filterData.whitelist = {}
    end
end

runtimeState.loadFilterWhitelist()
isAnimalInWhitelist = function(animalName6)
    if not filterData.enabled then
        return true
    end

    if #filterData.whitelist == 0 then
        return true
    end

    local text13 = string.lower(animalName6 or "")

    for _71, value38 in ipairs(filterData.whitelist) do
        local text14 = string.lower(value38)

        if text13 == text14 or string.find(text13, text14) or string.find(text14, text13) then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

runtimeState.findAnimalSearchMatches = function(query)
    if query then
        if not (#query < 3) then
            local text15 = string.lower(query)
            local entries35 = {}
            local allAnimalNames = getAllAnimalNames()

            if #allAnimalNames == 0 then
                print("⚠️ No se encontraron nombres en Brainrot...")

                if runtimeState.BrainrotSizes then
                    runtimeState.BrainrotSizes:IsA("Folder")
                end

                return entries35
            end

            for _72, name4 in ipairs(allAnimalNames) do
                local text16 = string.lower(name4)

                if string.find(text16, text15, 1, true) then
                    local model = runtimeState.BrainrotAssets.getModel(name4)

                    if model then
                        table.insert(entries35, {
                            name = name4,
                            model = model,
                            matchScore = #text15 / #text16,
                        })
                    end
                end
            end

            local function compareAnimalSearchMatches(firstMatch, secondMatch)
                return firstMatch.matchScore > secondMatch.matchScore
            end

            table.sort(entries35, compareAnimalSearchMatches)

            return entries35
        end
    end

    return {}
end

openFilterWindow = function()
    if runtimeState.isMainMenuOpen then
        runtimeState.isMainMenuOpen = false
        runtimeState.parent16.Visible = false
    end

    if runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen and runtimeState.parent11 then
        runtimeState.parent11:Destroy()
        runtimeState.parent11 = nil
        runtimeState.isPvpFinderOpen = false
    end

    if runtimeState.isTradeFinderOpen and runtimeState.parent12 then
        runtimeState.parent12:Destroy()
        runtimeState.parent12 = nil
        runtimeState.isTradeFinderOpen = false
    end

    if runtimeState.isItemSelectionOpen and runtimeState.parent13 then
        runtimeState.parent13:Destroy()
        runtimeState.parent13 = nil
        runtimeState.isItemSelectionOpen = false

        return
    end

    runtimeState.isItemSelectionOpen = true
    runtimeState.parent13 = Instance.new("Frame")
    runtimeState.parent13.Size = UDim2.new(0, 280, 0, 320)
    runtimeState.parent13.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -140, 0.5, -160)
    runtimeState.parent13.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 25)
    runtimeState.parent13.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    runtimeState.parent13.BorderSizePixel = 0

    getAllAnimalNames = function()
        local entries36 = {}

        if not runtimeState.BrainrotSizes then
            warn("⚠️ BrainrotSizes no encontrado")

            return entries36
        end

        for _76, child25 in ipairs(runtimeState.BrainrotSizes:GetChildren()) do
            table.insert(entries36, child25.Name)
        end

        return entries36
    end

    displaySearchResults = function(query2)
        local result123 = string.gsub(query2, "^%s*(.-)%s*$", "%1")

        clearAnimalSearchResults()

        local EmptyMessage3 = resultsScrollingFrame:FindFirstChild("EmptyMessage")

        if EmptyMessage3 then
            EmptyMessage3:Destroy()
        end

        if #result123 < 3 then
            if #filterData.whitelist ~= 0 then
                local entries37 = {}

                for _77, name5 in ipairs(filterData.whitelist) do
                    local model2 = runtimeState.BrainrotAssets.getModel(name5)

                    if model2 then
                        table.insert(entries37, {
                            name = name5,
                            model = model2,
                        })
                    end
                end

                table.sort(entries37, function(value67, value68)
                    return string.lower(value67.name) < string.lower(value68.name)
                end)

                local whitelistTitle = Instance.new("Frame")

                whitelistTitle.Name = "WhitelistTitle"
                whitelistTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
                whitelistTitle.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                whitelistTitle.LayoutOrder = 1
                whitelistTitle.Parent = resultsScrollingFrame

                local counter12 = 2
                local combinedValue4 = 30
                local textLabel19 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

                textLabel19.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                textLabel19.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                textLabel19.Text = "📋 MY WHITELIST (" .. #entries37 .. " animals)"
                textLabel19.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(100, 200, 255)
                textLabel19.TextSize = 12
                textLabel19.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                textLabel19.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                textLabel19.Parent = whitelistTitle

                local frame4 = Instance.new("Frame")

                frame4.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 1)
                frame4.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -5)
                frame4.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
                frame4.BorderSizePixel = 0
                frame4.Parent = whitelistTitle

                for _78, value40 in ipairs(entries37) do
                    createFrame7(value40, true, counter12)
                    counter12 += 1
                    combinedValue4 = combinedValue4 + 75 + 6
                end

                resultsScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, combinedValue4 + 10)

                return
            end

            local emptyMessage3 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

            emptyMessage3.Name = "EmptyMessage"
            emptyMessage3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
            emptyMessage3.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -20)
            emptyMessage3.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            emptyMessage3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            emptyMessage3.Text =
                "📋 No animals in whitelist yet.\n🔍 Type at least 3 letters to search..."
            emptyMessage3.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
            emptyMessage3.TextSize = 11
            emptyMessage3.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            emptyMessage3.TextWrapped = true
            emptyMessage3.ZIndex = 5000
            emptyMessage3.Parent = resultsScrollingFrame
            resultsScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 60)

            return
        end

        local animalSearchMatches = runtimeState.findAnimalSearchMatches(result123)

        if #animalSearchMatches == 0 then
            local emptyMessage4 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

            emptyMessage4.Name = "EmptyMessage"
            emptyMessage4.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
            emptyMessage4.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -20)
            emptyMessage4.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            emptyMessage4.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            emptyMessage4.Text = "❌ No animals found for: " .. result123
            emptyMessage4.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
            emptyMessage4.TextSize = 12
            emptyMessage4.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
            emptyMessage4.TextWrapped = true
            emptyMessage4.ZIndex = 5000
            emptyMessage4.Parent = resultsScrollingFrame
            resultsScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 50)

            return
        end

        local entries38 = {}
        local entries39 = {}

        for _79, value41 in ipairs(animalSearchMatches) do
            local isEnabled17 = false

            for _80, value42 in ipairs(filterData.whitelist) do
                if string.lower(value42) == string.lower(value41.name) then
                    isEnabled17 = true

                    break
                end
            end

            if isEnabled17 then
                table.insert(entries38, value41)
            else
                table.insert(entries39, value41)
            end
        end

        table.sort(entries38, function(value69, value70)
            return string.lower(value69.name) < string.lower(value70.name)
        end)
        table.sort(entries39, function(value71, value72)
            return string.lower(value71.name) < string.lower(value72.name)
        end)

        local combinedValue5 = 0
        local layoutOrder = 1

        local function createFrame24(title2, layoutOrder5)
            local parent107 = Instance.new("Frame")

            parent107.Name = "Separator_" .. title2
            parent107.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 25)
            parent107.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            parent107.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder5
            parent107.Parent = resultsScrollingFrame

            local frame5 = Instance.new("Frame")

            frame5.Size = UDim2.new(0.9, 0, 0, 1)
            frame5.Position = UDim2.new(0.05, 0, 0.5, 0)
            frame5.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
            frame5.BorderSizePixel = 0
            frame5.Parent = parent107

            local textLabel20 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

            textLabel20.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            textLabel20.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 0)
            textLabel20.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            textLabel20.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            textLabel20.Text = title2
            textLabel20.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
            textLabel20.TextSize = 10
            textLabel20.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            textLabel20.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            textLabel20.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
            textLabel20.Parent = parent107
            textLabel20.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -textLabel20.TextBounds.X / 2, 0, -8)

            return parent107
        end

        if #entries38 > 0 then
            createFrame24("📋 IN WHITELIST (" .. #entries38 .. ")", layoutOrder)
            layoutOrder += 1
            combinedValue5 += 25

            for _81, value43 in ipairs(entries38) do
                createFrame7(value43, true, layoutOrder)
                layoutOrder += 1
                combinedValue5 = combinedValue5 + 75 + 6
            end

            if #entries39 > 0 then
                local spacer = Instance.new("Frame")

                spacer.Name = "Spacer"
                spacer.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 10)
                spacer.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                spacer.LayoutOrder = layoutOrder
                spacer.Parent = resultsScrollingFrame
                layoutOrder += 1
                combinedValue5 += 10
            end
        end

        if #entries39 > 0 then
            createFrame24("🔍 OTHER RESULTS (" .. #entries39 .. ")", layoutOrder)

            local combinedValue6 = layoutOrder + 1

            combinedValue5 += 25

            for _82, value44 in ipairs(entries39) do
                createFrame7(value44, false, combinedValue6)
                combinedValue6 += 1
                combinedValue5 = combinedValue5 + 75 + 6
            end
        end

        resultsScrollingFrame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, combinedValue5 + 10)
    end

    local timestamp = 0

    parent100.Changed:Connect(function(value73)
        if value73 == "Text" then
            timestamp = tick()
            task.delay(0.3, function()
                if tick() - timestamp >= 0.29 then
                    displaySearchResults(parent100.Text)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        displaySearchResults("")
    end)
    parent99.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        filterData.enabled = not filterData.enabled
        parent99.Text = filterData.enabled and "✓ Filter ON" or "✗ Filter OFF"
        parent99.TextColor3 = filterData.enabled and Color3.fromRGB(40, 167, 69)
            or Color3.fromRGB(220, 53, 69)
        showNotification(
            filterData.enabled and "✅ Filter Enabled" or "❌ Filter Disabled",
            filterData.enabled and "Showing only whitelisted animals" or "Showing all animals",
            2
        )
    end)
    limpiarButton2.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        filterData.whitelist = {}
        runtimeState.saveFilterWhitelist()
        showNotification("🗑️ Whitelist Cleared", "All animals removed from whitelist", 3)

        if #parent100.Text >= 3 then
            displaySearchResults(parent100.Text)
        end
    end)

    local isEnabled18 = false
    local Position6
    local Position26

    whitelistManagerLabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input7)
        if input7.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            isEnabled18 = true
            Position6 = input7.Position
            Position26 = runtimeState.parent13.Position

            local signalConnection13 = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input29)
                if isEnabled18 then
                    if input29.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                        local offset9 = input29.Position - Position6

                        runtimeState.parent13.Position = UDim2.new(
                            Position26.X.Scale,
                            Position26.X.Offset + offset9.X,
                            Position26.Y.Scale,
                            Position26.Y.Offset + offset9.Y
                        )
                    end
                end
            end)
            local signalConnection14

            signalConnection14 = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input210)
                if input210.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    isEnabled18 = false
                    signalConnection13:Disconnect()
                    signalConnection14:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    runtimeState.parent13.Destroying:Connect(function()
        runtimeState.isItemSelectionOpen = false
        runtimeState.parent13 = nil
    end)
end

runtimeState.isEnabled19 = false
runtimeState.isEnabled20 = false

topRichList = {}

runtimeState.publishWealthLeaderboardEntry = function(animalData)
    if not animalData then
        return false
    end

    local userIdSettings = {
        userId = LocalPlayer.UserId,
        username = LocalPlayer.Name,
        animalName = animalData.name,
        animalDisplayName = animalData.displayName,
        animalValue = runtimeState.NumberUtils:ToString(animalData.value),
        animalValueRaw = animalData.value,
        animalRarity = animalData.rarity,
        animalMutation = animalData.mutation,
        animalTraits = animalData.traits,
    }
    local ok26, result124 = pcall(function()
        return request({
            Url = TOP_RICH_URL,
            Method = "POST",
            Headers = {
                ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
            },
            Body = HttpService:JSONEncode(userIdSettings),
        })
    end)

    if not ok26 or not result124 or result124.StatusCode ~= 200 then
        return false
    end

    local ok27, result214 = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(result124.Body)
    end)

    if ok27 and result214 then
        if result214.rank then
            showNotification(
                "👑 Top Rich",
                string.format(
                    "Rank #%d | Value: %s",
                    result214.rank,
                    runtimeState.NumberUtils:ToString(animalData.value)
                ),
                4
            )
        end
    end

    return true
end

getTopRichFromAPI = function()
    local ok28, result125 = pcall(function()
        return request({
            Url = TOP_RICH_URL,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = {
                ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
                ["Cache-Control"] = "no-cache",
            },
        })
    end)

    if ok28 and result125 and result125.StatusCode == 200 then
        local ok29, result215 = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(result125.Body)
        end)

        if ok29 and result215 then
            return result215
        end
    end

    return {}
end
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(5)

    if runtimeState.isEnabled19 or runtimeState.isEnabled20 then
        return
    end

    runtimeState.isEnabled20 = true

    local mostValuableAnimal = getMostValuableAnimal()

    if mostValuableAnimal then
        runtimeState.publishWealthLeaderboardEntry(mostValuableAnimal)
        runtimeState.isEnabled19 = true
    end

    runtimeState.isEnabled20 = false
end)

    updateTopRichList = function()
        for _84, child26 in ipairs(topRichScrollingFrame:GetChildren()) do
            if
                child26:IsA("Frame")
                and child26.Name ~= "MainLayout"
                and child26.Name ~= "FramePadding"
            then
                child26:Destroy()
            end
        end

        local wealthLeaderboard2 = runtimeState.wealthLeaderboard

        if not wealthLeaderboard2 or #wealthLeaderboard2 == 0 then
            if runtimeState.isWebSocketSupported and wsConnections.toprich then
                wealthLeaderboard2 = runtimeState.wealthLeaderboard or {}
            else
                wealthLeaderboard2 = getTopRichFromAPI()
            end
        end

        local EmptyMessage4 = topRichScrollingFrame:FindFirstChild("EmptyMessage")

        if EmptyMessage4 then
            EmptyMessage4:Destroy()
        end

        if wealthLeaderboard2 and #wealthLeaderboard2 ~= 0 then
            for i17, value45 in ipairs(wealthLeaderboard2) do
                if i17 <= 24 then
                    createFrame8(value45, i17, i17)
                end
            end

            return
        end

        local emptyMessage5 = Instance.new("TextLabel")

        emptyMessage5.Name = "EmptyMessage"
        emptyMessage5.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        emptyMessage5.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, -20)
        emptyMessage5.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
        emptyMessage5.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        emptyMessage5.Text = "No hay jugadores en el Top Rich"
        emptyMessage5.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 150)
        emptyMessage5.TextSize = 11
        emptyMessage5.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        emptyMessage5.TextWrapped = true
        emptyMessage5.ZIndex = 1000
        emptyMessage5.Parent = topRichScrollingFrame
    end
    updateTopRichList()

    local isEnabled21 = false
    local Position7
    local Position27

    topRICHLabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input8)
        if input8.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
            isEnabled21 = true
            Position7 = input8.Position
            Position27 = runtimeState.parent14.Position

            local signalConnection15 = UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input211)
                if isEnabled21 then
                    if input211.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement then
                        local offset10 = input211.Position - Position7

                        runtimeState.parent14.Position = UDim2.new(
                            Position27.X.Scale,
                            Position27.X.Offset + offset10.X,
                            Position27.Y.Scale,
                            Position27.Y.Offset + offset10.Y
                        )
                    end
                end
            end)
            local signalConnection16

            signalConnection16 = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input212)
                if input212.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    isEnabled21 = false
                    signalConnection15:Disconnect()
                    signalConnection16:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    runtimeState.parent14.Destroying:Connect(function()
        runtimeState.isLeaderboardOpen = false
        runtimeState.parent14 = nil
    end)
end

                        Position28.X.Offset + offset11.X,
                        Position28.Y.Scale,
                        Position28.Y.Offset + offset11.Y
                    )
                end
            end)
            local signalConnection18

                if input214.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    isEnabled22 = false
                    signalConnection17:Disconnect()
                    signalConnection18:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end
    end)

        runtimeState.isPremiumWindowOpen = false
        runtimeState.parent15 = nil

runtimeState.playerStatisticsCache = {}

runtimeState.getPlayerDuelStatistics = function(username10)
    if not username10 or username10 == "" then
        return nil
    end

    local result128 = runtimeState.playerStatisticsCache[username10]

    if result128 and tick() - result128.timestamp < 60 then
        return result128.data
    end

    local ok30, result129 = pcall(function()
        return request({
            Url = "https://mbpymdkvxncpwrpfxzkx.supabase.co/rest/v1/duel_stats"
                .. "?username=eq."
                .. username10,
            Method = "GET",
            Headers = {
                apikey = "sb_publishable_lLcR1AVcxE40aIF-Y4RLtQ_6xOGMa4k",
                Authorization = "Bearer sb_publishable_lLcR1AVcxE40aIF-Y4RLtQ_6xOGMa4k",
                ["Content-Type"] = "application/json",
            },
        })
    end)

    if not ok30 or not result129 or result129.StatusCode ~= 200 then
        return nil
    end

    local ok210, result216 = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(result129.Body)
    end)

    if not ok210 or not result216 or #result216 == 0 then
        return nil
    end

    runtimeState.playerStatisticsCache[username10] = {
        data = result216[1],
        timestamp = tick(),
    }

    return result216[1]
end
runtimeState.formatPlayerDuelStatistics = function(statistics)
    if not statistics then
        return nil
    end

    if statistics.streak and statistics.streak >= 5 then
        return string.format(' <font color="#00CCFF">🔥 %d Racha</font>', statistics.streak)
    end

    local wins = statistics.wins or 0

    return string.format(
        ' | <font color="#00FF00">%dW</font>/<font color="#FF0000"> %dL</font>',
        wins,
        statistics.losses or 0
    )
end

task.spawn(function()
    while true do
        task.wait(300)

        for key5, value46 in pairs(runtimeState.playerStatisticsCache) do
            if not (tick() - value46.timestamp > 60) then
                continue
            end

            runtimeState.playerStatisticsCache[key5] = nil
        end
    end
end)

runtimeState.previousPlayerStatusUpdater = runtimeState.updatePlayerStatusLabel

runtimeState.updatePlayerStatusLabel = function(label2, username11, userId9)
    runtimeState.previousPlayerStatusUpdater(label2, username11, userId9)

    if runtimeState.parent11 and runtimeState.parent11.Parent then
        task.spawn(function()
            local playerDuelStatistics = runtimeState.getPlayerDuelStatistics(username11)

            if playerDuelStatistics and label2 and label2.Parent then
                local text17 = runtimeState.formatPlayerDuelStatistics(playerDuelStatistics)

                if text17 then
                    label2.RichText = true

                    if not string.find(label2.Text, tostring(playerDuelStatistics.wins)) then
                        local StatsLabel = label2.Parent:FindFirstChild("StatsLabel")

                        if not StatsLabel then
                            StatsLabel = label2:Clone()
                            StatsLabel.Name = "StatsLabel"
                            StatsLabel.Parent = label2.Parent
                            StatsLabel.RichText = true
                            StatsLabel.Position = UDim2.new(
                                label2.Position.X.Scale,
                                label2.Position.X.Offset,
                                label2.Position.Y.Scale,
                                label2.Position.Y.Offset + 20
                            )
                            StatsLabel.TextScaled = false
                            StatsLabel.TextSize = math.floor(label2.TextSize * 0.9)
                        end

                        StatsLabel.Text = text17
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

runtimeState.reconnectAttempts = 0
runtimeState.isReconnecting = false

    runtimeState.reconnectBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if runtimeState.isReconnecting then

        end

        manualReconnect()

updateConnectionStatus = function(isConnected, reason, showReconnect)
    if not runtimeState.statusLabel2 then
        return
    end

    if isConnected then
        runtimeState.statusLabel2.Text = "✅ Conectado"
        runtimeState.statusLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 167, 69)

        if runtimeState.reconnectBtn then
            runtimeState.reconnectBtn.Visible = false
        end

        runtimeState.reconnectAttempts = 0
    else
        local text18 = "❌ Desconectado"

        if reason then
            text18 ..= " - " .. reason
        end

        runtimeState.statusLabel2.Text = text18
        runtimeState.statusLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 53, 69)

        if
            not showReconnect and (not reason or not string.find(string.lower(reason), "cerrado"))
        then
            if runtimeState.reconnectBtn then
                runtimeState.reconnectBtn.Visible = false
            end
        elseif runtimeState.reconnectBtn then
            runtimeState.reconnectBtn.Visible = true

            local offset12 = 3 - runtimeState.reconnectAttempts

            if offset12 > 0 then
                runtimeState.reconnectBtn.Text = "RECONNECT (" .. offset12 .. ")"
            else
                runtimeState.reconnectBtn.Text = "RESET"
            end
        end
    end
end

attemptReconnect = function()
    if runtimeState.isReconnecting then
        return false
    end

    if runtimeState.reconnectAttempts >= 3 then
        updateConnectionStatus(
            false,
            "Máximo de intentos alcanzado. Click RECONNECT para resetear.",
            true
        )
        showNotification(
            "❌ Conexión Perdida",
            "No se pudo reconectar después de 3 intentos.\nPresiona RECONNECT para reintentar.",
            5,
            true
        )

        return false
    end

    runtimeState.isReconnecting = true
    runtimeState.reconnectAttempts += 1

    local offset13 = 3 - runtimeState.reconnectAttempts

    updateConnectionStatus(
        false,
        "Reconectando... (" .. runtimeState.reconnectAttempts .. "/3)",
        false
    )
    showNotification(
        "🔄 Reconectando",
        "Intento "
            .. runtimeState.reconnectAttempts
            .. " de "
            .. 3
            .. (offset13 > 0 and " (" .. offset13 .. " restantes)" or " - Último intento"),
        2
    )

    if runtimeState.socketConnection then
        pcall(function()
            if runtimeState.socketConnection.Close then
                runtimeState.socketConnection:Close()
            elseif runtimeState.socketConnection.close then
                runtimeState.socketConnection:close()
            end
        end)
        runtimeState.socketConnection = nil
    end

    runtimeState.isSocketConnected = false
    runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated = false
    task.wait(2)

    local result1292 = runtimeState.connectSocket()

    runtimeState.isReconnecting = false

    if result1292 then
        updateConnectionStatus(true)
        showNotification("✅ Reconectado", "Conexión restablecida exitosamente", 3)

        return true
    end

    if runtimeState.reconnectAttempts >= 3 then
        updateConnectionStatus(false, "Máximo alcanzado. Click RECONNECT para resetear.", true)

        if runtimeState.reconnectBtn then
            runtimeState.reconnectBtn.Text = "RESET"
        end
    else
        updateConnectionStatus(
            false,
            "Intento fallido. Quedan " .. 3 - runtimeState.reconnectAttempts .. " intentos.",
            false
        )
    end

    return false
end

task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(1)
    runtimeState.createConnectionStatusDisplay()
    runtimeState.connectSocket()

    if runtimeState.isWebSocketSupported then
        updateConnectionStatus(
            runtimeState.isSocketConnected and runtimeState.isSocketAuthenticated,
            nil,
            false
        )
    else
        updateConnectionStatus(false, "WebSocket no disponible", true)
        showNotification("⚠️ Error", "WebSocket no disponible. Actualiza tu executor.", 5, true)
    end
end)
